# UMCD-1: Unified Multipath Channel Dataset — Complete Design, Generation, Validation, and Research Plan

**Edition 3.667 G3 frozen path-coefficient criterion is now scored (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Contract-bound raw path comparison now applies the frozen `coefficient_relative_tolerance` to matched receiver-relevant path classes using `|a-b| / max(|a|, |b|, tiny)`, where each coefficient is the coherent aggregate within the frozen geometric class. A tolerance miss fails the episode alongside the existing 95% bidirectional relevance-coverage criterion. Contract-bound creation and run-manifest replay both use the exact frozen tolerance; the report names the active threshold. A regression confirms a coefficient mismatch fails despite full geometric coverage. The four focused G3 path/joint-evaluator modules pass 123 tests and targeted Ruff checks pass. Direct diagnostic runs without a frozen contract remain diagnostic and do not infer this margin; all G3 outputs remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`.

**Edition 3.666 G4 rotated LOS covariance orientation family expanded (2026-10-26, implementation pending native qualification):** Extended the ETSI-derived CDL-D/E two-element dual-VH covariance test from one to three UT Euler orientations: mixed-axis `(0.37, 0.28, -0.41)`, pure yaw `(0, 0, 0.65)`, and roll/pitch `(0.28, -0.36, 0)` rad. The frozen Bonferroni 99% family now covers 96 real Hermitian components with six distinct seeds at 16,384 draws/profile/orientation; per-claim half-width remains bounded by 0.05 normalized mean power. Protocol and regression definitions agree. Native execution and seed-collision audit remain deferred; this finite orientation set does not establish continuous orientation, mutual coupling, or G4 qualification.

**Edition 3.665 G5 acceptance logic isolated from receiver runtime (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Moved bounded SNR-family contract freezing and simultaneous-interval scoring into a Torch-free module, preserving the existing acceptance semantics and adding explicit overflow rejection for nonrepresentable numeric values. Its dependency-isolated suite passes 17 tests across roster drift, minimum cluster floor, PASS/FAIL/NOT_EVALUABLE, and malformed interval bounds; Ruff passes on the touched Python modules. No receiver simulation ran; the independent-channel floor is still only an executable minimum, and scientific/field qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.664 G3 preregistration identity canonicalization (2026-10-26, focused validation):** The frozen G3 run-contract builder now rejects padded task, scenario, candidate/challenger, waveform, episode, path-roster, and per-stratum identity strings instead of trimming selected path-roster values or admitting aliases. The qualification-contract module passes 33 focused tests, including seven padded-identity regressions. This secures canonical contract identity only; no scientific evidence or G3 qualification is established, and production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.663 G5 bounded curve runner import repair (2026-10-26, implementation pending environment validation):** Fixed a malformed evidence-staging indentation that prevented importing the bounded ordered-impairment SNR-curve runner and corrected scope text assembly that Python interpreted as a string call. The Torch-free G5 independent-channel statistics module passes 4 tests. The curve schema module remains uncollected because the current Halti `.venv` does not contain Torch; no receiver data generation or qualification was run.

**Edition 3.662 G3 contract-bound joint evaluator focused integration (2026-10-26):** Updated stale regression fixtures to the current qualification-contract v4/input-v6 boundary and corrected assertions to test fail-closed input admission, exact contract-budget binding, and runtime identity in the run manifest. The joint-evaluation and evidence-artifact modules pass 73 focused tests. Together with the 127-test canonical identity/schema slice and 27-test signed-lock/contract slice, this is targeted software evidence only; material acquisition, independent measurement truth, external trust, scientific qualification, production RT, and advanced training remain gated.

**Edition 3.661 G4 rotated LOS dual-polarized covariance reference definition (2026-10-26, implementation pending native qualification):** Added a source-hash-bound CDL-D/E two-element dual-VH receive covariance definition for UT orientation `(0.37, 0.28, -0.41)` rad. The expected matrix composes the rotated receive polarization basis, rotated local-y ULA steering, independent diffuse ray covariance, and deterministic LOS K-factor term from ETSI-derived inputs. A frozen 16,384-draw/profile, 32-claim 99% family and distinct seed roster are recorded in the test/protocol. The native experiment and seed-collision audit remain deferred; this closes only the missing definition for one orientation, not G4.

**Edition 3.660 G3 path diagnostic schema synchronized with diffuse-path reporting (2026-10-26):** Advanced the path-equivalence roster report to diagnostic schema v2 and added a strict schema for raw-input digests, optional diffuse distribution summaries, frozen solver-budget provenance, descriptive-only status, and limitations. The source bundle already named the v2 schema; the regression exposed that the artifact shape had not been fully specified. Targeted changed-module validation passes 127 tests, including all path-roster tests. This is schema/software integrity evidence only; diffuse summaries remain descriptive, finite solver caps are not truth, G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and no native G4/G5 qualification was run.

**Edition 3.659 G1 independent segment-visibility oracle foundation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `propagation.visibility.segment_mesh_visibility`, an independent triangle-surface segment classifier with visible/blocked/ambiguous outcomes, explicit meter scaling, affine mesh transform support, and conservative edge/vertex/endpoint/coplanar outcomes. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. This is not yet a production mesh-inventory adapter and does not resolve thin-wall material transmission, scene transforms/units from native assets, or OSM-scene geometric truth; no tests, Ruff, formatting, type checks, or native qualification runs have been performed, and no gate is closed.

**Edition 3.658 G7 split identity enforcement through the legacy model runner (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** ChannelDataset group and child-binding paths preserve canonical split identities without trimming, and the signed-split experiment resolver rejects padded subject/group IDs. Regression definitions cover dataset admission, parent/child binding, and runner resolution. Model-input data remain separated from metadata/targets; the complete downstream adapter audit remains open. No tests or static checks have run; release qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.657 G7 legacy split identity canonicalization (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The legacy independent-split builder, assignment object, and leakage auditor now fail closed on padded sample/group IDs, seeds, and configured identity field names. Regression definitions cover the builder, frozen assignment, and audit path. Canonical string inputs keep the existing deterministic assignment rule; no validation has run and no dataset release is qualified.

**Edition 3.656 G3 identity-boundary completion (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The paired receiver scorer now rejects padded family identities in expected strata and observed records, and raw path-equivalence roster/configuration identities fail closed instead of being normalized. The interrupted receiver-scoring conditional is repaired. This is contract hardening only; validation is deferred, qualification remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and production D1 generation and advanced training remain gated.

**Edition 3.655 G5.14 DCI 0_3 TPMI width aggregation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Non-codebook cells now contribute zero bits, while codebook cells require their decoded per-cell TPMI width; the resolver aggregates by type1a maximum or type2 sum across selected cells. Regression definitions cover both modes and mixed scheduled cells. Direct codebook table-width derivation remains open; no validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.654 G5.14 DCI 0_3 antenna-port field width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver derives each cell's antenna-port width from its transform-precoding and DMRS configuration, combines mapping types by maximum, then applies the configured type1a maximum or type2 sum over selected cells. Regression definitions are authored; codebook precoding/layer-table widths remain open. Validation is deferred. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.653 G5.14 DCI 0_3 non-codebook SRI capability selection (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Non-codebook SRI width uses configured `maxMIMO-Layers` only when UE support for that parameter is declared; otherwise the resolver requires the UE-supported maximum layer count. Missing capability fails closed. Regression definitions cover configured, absent, unsupported-config, and missing-capability cases. No validation has run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.652 G5.14 DCI 0_3 SRS resource indicator width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver derives per-cell SRI widths from codebook/non-codebook configuration and configured SRS resources, using combinatorial subset counts for non-codebook transmission. Type1a aggregates by maximum; type2 aggregates by sum over the selected scheduled cells. Regression definitions cover both modes and malformed configuration; validation is deferred. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.651 G5.14 DCI 0_3 measurement-gap cancellation field (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added the Release 19 field after PDCCH monitoring adaptation in the ordered DCI 0_3 layout. It occupies one bit when configured and zero otherwise. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.650 G5.14 DCI 0_3 open-loop power-control indication width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-state resolver derives the field width from P0-PUSCH set-list presence, SRI field presence, and configured OLPC indication width, taking the maximum applicable width across selected cells. Regression definitions cover zero-, one-, and configured two-bit cases plus malformed input maps. Validation is deferred. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.649 G5.14 DCI 0_3 PTRS-DMRS association width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The resolver derives two bits per selected cell when PTRS-UplinkConfig is configured, transform precoding is disabled, and maximum PUSCH rank exceeds one; otherwise the cell contributes zero bits. Regression definitions cover selected-cell composition and the zero-width conditions. Validation remains deferred. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.648 G5.14 DCI 0_3 SCell dormancy width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-state resolver derives the dormancy bitmap width from the configured group IDs and DCI occasion context. The field is absent outside the specified primary-cell, DRX Active Time, and multiple-SCell-DL-BWP conditions; applicable configurations require one to five unique group IDs. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.647 G5.14 DCI 0_3 PDCCH monitoring adaptation width (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-configuration resolver derives the PDCCH monitoring adaptation field width from the enable state and configured PDCCH skipping-duration/search-space group lists, including the Release 19 zero-, one-, and two-bit cases. Regression definitions cover these paths and invalid configurations; validation is deferred. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, clause 7.3.1.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.646 G5.14 DCI 0_3 frequency-domain assignment widths (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The decoded-state field-width resolver now derives each scheduled cell's frequency-assignment width from active UL BWP PRB extent and resource-allocation configuration, including type 0, type 1, dynamic switch, type-1 granularity/hopping offsets, and interlace allocation. Widths are summed over the selected scheduled-cell combination. Regression definitions cover mode formulas, combined-cell widths, and malformed BWP configuration. The mapping follows ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0 clause 7.3.1.1.4 and TS 38.214 V19.2.0 clauses 6.1.2.2.1–6.1.2.2.3; execution remains deferred. ASN.1/PER, CRC/polar decoding, other DCI widths, and receiver qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.588 G5 network PHY power/noise and composition integrity (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Multi-transmitter grid composition and SINR accounting now reject Boolean/complex/sparse/nonnumeric power and noise inputs before coercion, reject power/noise scales not representable in the receiver dtype, and reject nonfinite scaling/channel products. SINR and receiver-noise paths verify that the recorded network grid equals the sum of its source contributions. Regression definitions cover malformed inputs, scale boundaries, and inconsistent composition; no tests were run. This hardens a synchronized frequency-domain baseband primitive and does not model timing/CFO, RF effects, link budget/beam truth, or qualify a multi-cell receiver.

**Edition 3.587 G4 joint delay/angle expectations use independent CDL source rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The all-profile four-angle characteristic and joint delay/angle regressions now derive cluster powers, delays, and angles from the ETSI-transcribed CDL fixture. The native Sionna ray generation remains the system under test, and its package-table digest remains provenance only. This removes shared parameter inputs from these joint-distribution expectations but does not qualify all angular laws, antenna patterns, polarization, or physical-channel behavior; no tests were run.

**Edition 3.586 G4 statistical expectations use independent CDL source rows (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The all-profile A–E joint TX/RX spatial covariance reference and 3D-velocity temporal covariance reference now derive cluster powers and angles from the ETSI-transcribed per-cluster fixture; Sionna package JSON is retained only for implementation table-digest provenance. The expectations retain the independent ray-offset/coupling calculations and confidence bounds already declared by those regressions. This removes a shared-parameter-source weakness but does not qualify other array/pattern/covariance families, all temporal regimes, or physical-channel behavior; no tests were run.

**Edition 3.585 G4 profile-version source coverage (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The exact ETSI source manifest now pins TR 38.901 v16.1.0 as well as v19.2.0 and TS 38.101-4 v19.2.2. A regression definition joins every version declared by the standardized TDL/CDL applicability ledger to a pinned source and explicitly rejects the unsupported SCM/UMi family. The manifest remains identity-only; this does not qualify profile conformance or broaden supported models.

**Edition 3.585 G4 profile-version source coverage (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The exact ETSI source manifest now pins TR 38.901 v16.1.0 as well as v19.2.0 and TS 38.101-4 v19.2.2. A regression definition joins every version declared by the standardized TDL/CDL applicability ledger to a pinned source and explicitly rejects the unsupported SCM/UMi family. The manifest remains identity-only; this does not qualify profile conformance or broaden supported models.

**Edition 3.579 G4 fixed-TDL PDP RMS recomputation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The native regression definition now recomputes RMS delay spread from resolved per-cluster delays and mean powers, then checks that independent calculation against the adapter-reported scalar and pinned ETSI value with a tolerance derived from the standard's 0.1 dB tap-power rounding. This closes the self-reported-scalar-only weakness in the code path. It still does not independently compare every tap power or qualify fading/statistical behavior; no tests were run.

**Edition 3.576 G3 scorer implementation inventory (2026-10-26, code present; consolidated validation and scientific admission pending):** Reconciled checklist status with the implemented material-calibration, complex-CSI, propagation-metrics, and paired NR receiver scorers, their common-family joint evaluator, and acquisition-design assurance path. Regression suites have not been run in this implementation pass. External measurement/runtime trust, representative independent acquisition units, preregistration evidence, and scientific acceptance remain open; outputs stay `NOT_QUALIFIED`.

**Edition 3.575 G4 fixed-TDL tap-summary references (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added hash-bound Annex B tap-count, maximum-excess-delay, and delay-resolution summaries for all seven fixed TDL profiles. Native regression definitions compare these properties with each resolved Sionna profile in addition to the existing RMS-delay scalars. The compact fixture omits full tap powers, fading distributions, and statistical behavior; validation is deferred.

**Edition 3.574 G5.09 bounded receiver workload (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The independent-channel ordered-impairment runner rejects requests above 16,384 transport blocks before constructing its Sionna receiver. Regression definitions verify the preflight guard. The ceiling bounds computation only; the runner remains a single-operating-point synthetic fixture and this does not establish G5 qualification. No checks or receiver runs have been made.

**Edition 3.573 G7 bounded NR trajectory reads (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** NR trajectory pointer manifests now use stable single-link reads capped at 1 MiB, and trajectory JSON content is capped at 64 MiB; the writer enforces the same limits before publication. Regression definitions cover over-limit manifest/content inputs, hard-linked manifests, early rejection before content access, and writer-side refusal. No validation has run. This extends bounded input handling but does not satisfy measured end-to-end RSS or cross-format retry/failure requirements.

**Edition 3.572 G7 bounded NR manifest inventory reads (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The NR sharded iterator's manifest scan now reads each resolved manifest through a stable descriptor with a 1 MiB cap, checks regular-file/single-link identity before and after reading, and rejects oversize input before record-payload estimation. It retains the existing SQLite-backed deterministic inventory and symlink-alias duplicate detection. Regression definitions cover oversized and hard-linked manifests; no validation has run. This closes an input-bound gap, not the remaining cross-format retry/failure or measured-RSS requirements.

**Edition 3.571 G4 fixed-TDL independent RMS scalar references (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a compact, source-hash-bound reference for the Annex B RMS delay-spread scalars of fixed TDL A10/A30/B100/C60/C300/D10/D30. Native regression definitions compare each emitted profile metadata value with its ETSI scalar and bind the fixture to the pinned TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 source identity. The reference omits the normative tap tables and makes no statistical conformance claim; validation remains deferred.

**Edition 3.570 G3 paired NR receiver evaluator contract hardening (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Reconciled the backlog with the existing `g3_receiver_impact` scorer and its contract-bound joint-evaluator integration, which already provide paired common-SNR BLER effects, cluster-bootstrap intervals, bracketed SNR@10% shifts without extrapolation, transport-block precision, and receiver configuration-row digest binding. Hardened direct evaluator inputs so malformed/unhashable configuration IDs, LOS/NLOS labels, and expected strata fail with explicit contract errors. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. Numerical outputs remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`; this does not establish receiver execution truth or external scientific authority.

**Edition 3.569 G5.09 exact receiver CRC accounting (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The independent-channel ordered-impairment producer now counts CRC failures directly from the retained per-block Boolean outcomes and computes block BLER from that exact integer numerator. A regression definition cross-checks each condition against the complete channel/block roster. The report contract and frozen fixture criteria are unchanged; no receiver qualification is added, and no validation has run.

**Edition 3.568 G4 normative-reference source identity audit (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a versioned identity-only manifest for ETSI TR 38.901 v16.1.0/v19.2.0 and TS 38.101-4 v19.2.2 and a bounded CLI auditor for exact locally downloaded PDF bytes. Strict schemas define the manifest and audit report. The audit uses stable, bounded, single-link regular-file reads, rejects duplicate JSON keys and unsupported manifest fields, and reports source-byte success while retaining `NOT_QUALIFIED`. Regression definitions cover matching bytes, tampering, missing/unsafe inputs, manifest rejection, report-schema conformance, and CLI output; no validation has run. ETSI PDFs are not checked into the repository. This pins source identity only; extraction, independent parameter comparison, and G4 statistical conformance remain open.

**Edition 3.567 G5.14 same-grid SRS inter-cell occupancy audit (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a typed SRS resource-occurrence contract and bounded audit that expands resolved frequency allocations into slot-symbol-subcarrier coordinates on one caller-defined common grid. The versioned diagnostic reports exact cross-cell shared RE coordinates up to a fixed cap and aggregate pairwise resource overlap counts. Regression definitions cover overlapping resources, disjoint comb/slot resources, and incomparable grid contexts. It explicitly does not infer cyclic-shift orthogonality, RF interference, or receiver impact; full DCI/RRC, positioning-report integration, and end-to-end receiver qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.565 G3 diffuse-path distribution diagnostic primitive (2026-10-26, integration pending):** Added a descriptive summary/comparison primitive for stochastic diffuse path distributions, with fixed 5/50/95% delay, path-power, zenith, and circular azimuth-offset quantiles; circular azimuth-center deltas; and hash-bound solver-cap provenance. The primitive does not assign ray identities, define acceptance margins, or treat finite caps as truth. Regression definitions are authored but unrun. Versioned evaluator-input/report integration and contract-bound use remain open.

**Edition 3.560 G3 raw path-equivalence recomputation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added joint-evaluator input v5, which carries solver-valid deterministic candidate/challenger paths and full CFR components instead of a supplied comparison result. The evaluator builds effective path classes, recomputes one-to-one matches, coverage, and receiver relevance, and binds each raw episode payload by SHA-256. Qualification contract v4 freezes all settings used by that computation. New contract-bound runs require v4/input v5, validate the input schema before scoring, and bind both schemas in the source bundle; v3/input v4 remains available for historical diagnostics. Regression definitions cover raw recomputation, schema/source binding, receipt replay, and frozen-setting drift. No validation has run; outputs remain diagnostic-only and production RT remains gated.

**Edition 3.547 G7 NR target reason-code persistence (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `NRChannelRecord` requires zero-filled masked target elements and explicit elementwise reason codes consistent with each broadcasted mask. `NRTrajectory` exposes reason series, NR record format v2 persists compact codebook-index arrays, and the v1 reader preserves compatibility with the explicit `legacy_reason_unrecorded` reason while normalizing unavailable storage to zero. The RT trajectory adapter records `not_observable` for no-path CFR. Regression definitions cover contract errors and v1/v2 storage paths; validation remains deferred. This does not close the remaining G7 target semantics, lineage, corpus coverage, or release gates.

**Edition 3.546 G7 externally signed public-release manifest (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The ODbL exporter now refuses unsigned release manifests and requires exact-byte OpenSSH verification under a dedicated release namespace and caller-supplied external allowed-signers file. It places the original manifest bytes, detached signature, and a trust-digest approval receipt in the archive, and rejects duplicate JSON keys. Added a packaged approval receipt schema and regression definitions. This proves signer identity for the declaration only; OSM source provenance, live source offers, legal classification, third-party rights, and external trust-file governance remain unresolved, so public release stays gated. No checks have run.

**Edition 3.545 G7 strict ODbL release-manifest contract (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The public release validator now rejects unknown/missing top-level, scene, and artifact fields and requires lowercase artifact SHA-256 digests. Added `datasets/schema/public-release-manifest.v2.schema.json` as the packaged strict structural contract and regression definitions for unversioned fields, noncanonical hashes, and schema/manifest agreement. The exporter continues to bind per-artifact scene lineage and generate notices, but these changes do not recover upstream OSM provenance, establish legal classification, verify that source offers are live, or close third-party rights; public release remains gated. No checks have run.

**Edition 3.544 G3 receiver-bootstrap output allocation bound (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The paired receiver-impact scorer now bounds its retained bootstrap output arrays to 8,000,000 float64 cells and rejects oversized bootstrap-draw/SNR-grid combinations before allocating arrays. The diagnostic report includes both the computed cell requirement and cap; a regression definition covers rejection. This protects bounded evaluator execution without changing statistical criteria or qualifying G3. No validation has run.

**Edition 3.543 G5 PUSCH noise-variance input contract (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The PUSCH receiver now validates noise variance as a dense real numeric input, rejects Boolean/complex/negative/non-finite values, and verifies representability after float32 conversion. This prevents imaginary-part loss or accidental Boolean-as-one interpretation in the receiver. Dependency-isolated regression definitions cover invalid values, overflow, and accepted real inputs; no checks have run, and no G5 result or qualification status changes.

**Edition 3.542 G4 standardized adapter rejects non-real physical scalars (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** TDL/CDL generation now rejects Boolean and non-real physical values for carrier, subcarrier, and sampling frequencies; delay spread and speed; TDL LOS angle; CDL antenna spacing; and CDL orientation/velocity components before importing or initializing Sionna. Regression definitions cover representative Boolean and malformed-type inputs. No checks have run; this closes an input-contract gap only and makes no channel-model conformance claim.

**Edition 3.538 G4 TDL sinusoid-count forwarding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `generate_tdl_frequency_response()` now passes its configured `num_sinusoids` value into the pinned Sionna 2.2 `TDL` constructor; previously the adapter retained that value in metadata while Sionna silently used its default of 20. Added a dependency-isolated regression definition that captures constructor arguments and checks a non-default value is also recorded in metadata. No validation or statistical qualification is claimed. This is parameter plumbing; independent table/source references and TDL temporal-statistics qualification remain open. [Pinned Sionna 2.2 TDL API](https://github.com/NVlabs/sionna/blob/v2.2.0/src/sionna/phy/channel/tr38901/tdl.py).

**Edition 3.539 G4 packaged TDL table identity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Standardized TDL generation now hashes the exact version-selected packaged JSON resource: scalable profiles identify their TR 38.901 version; fixed profiles identify TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2. Standardized-record admission checks the canonical source label, validates the raw SHA-256 field, and requires the table identity/hash to be covered by the resolved-profile digest. Legacy rows without table identity remain compatible. Regression definitions cover raw resource-byte hashing, accepted binding, and source-hash tampering. This identifies the installed Sionna table and does not substitute for an independently sourced standards reference. No validation or qualification is claimed.

**Edition 3.540 G4 CDL packaged-table resource identity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** CDL generation now loads and hashes the selected TR 38.901 JSON using Sionna's package-resource `Traversable` interface, and records its canonical version/profile source label. This supports non-filesystem-backed package resources and makes the installed table identity explicit. A canonical binding digest covers profile, version, table name, source label, and raw hash; standardized-record admission recomputes it and validates the declared identity while preserving legacy metadata without a source label. Regression definitions cover raw resource bytes, valid admission, identity mismatch, and stale bindings. Independent table-value comparison and all validation remain open.

**Edition 3.541 G7 signed split-lock rejects provisional RT identities (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The saved-ChannelDataset audit now rejects signed split-registry comparison when any input RT parent carries a provisional scene-name identity. This prevents incomplete scene-asset closure from entering release-lock evidence while preserving the unsigned diagnostic path and its provisional-count report. A regression definition builds multiple provisional scene parents and expects rejection at the signed-lock boundary. No checks have run; authoritative inventory, external signer custody, and release qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.532 G5.14 decoded multi-cell SRS offset-combo scheduling (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported composition API that applies an already-decoded DCI 0_3 request-list row and SRS-OffsetCombo row across the full configured cell roster while accepting the DCI-selected co-scheduled subset separately. It resolves each triggered resource set's target slot and per-symbol frequency hops, preserving per-cell DCI-slot coordinates for differing numerologies. It fails closed when a selected set needs a missing/out-of-range offset entry. DCI/RRC decoding, scheduled-cell-combination selection, per-cell BWP selection, and independent mapping/receiver qualification remain outside this adapter; regression definitions are authored but unrun. Mapping follows [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Tables 7.3.1.1.4-4/-5](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf) and [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.02.00_60/ts_138331v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.533 G4 TDL resolved-profile integrity digest (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Generated TDL metadata now contains a canonical SHA-256 digest over profile identity/version, delay semantics and spread, LOS/K-factor state, and resolved nominal tap delays and powers. Standardized-record admission recomputes the digest when present and rejects changed or malformed declarations. Regression definitions cover metadata tampering. The digest binds the record's declared resolved table to itself; it is not an independent 3GPP source/table reference, does not hash the installed Sionna source, and is optional for legacy records. G4 source/reference inventory and consolidated validation remain open.
**Edition 3.534 G8 target-coverage independent-unit requirement (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `audit_target_coverage` now rejects a target record without an explicit `independent_group_id`; distinct record IDs are no longer treated as evidence of independent samples. The corpus auditor supplies each episode's frozen leakage-component ID. Regression definitions cover omitted identity, while the generic evaluator still relies on callers to provide reviewed grouping metadata. No checks have run; broader effective-sample-size and inference audits remain open.
**Edition 3.535 G3 run-manifest signer role policy (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The verifier can optionally authorize the cryptographically verified OpenSSH principal against a separate canonical policy containing organization, role, and the exact run-manifest verification purpose. The report includes policy identity/hash and role authorization; an absent policy is reported separately from signature verification. Added a versioned policy schema and regression definitions for authorized and wrong-purpose signers. Run receipts remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`; external policy custody/governance and scientific qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.536 G7.08 bounded split-audit manifests (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The stable regular-file reader now accepts a byte ceiling and enforces it against the opened descriptor before reading and while streaming, preventing oversized child-binding rosters, frozen inventories, and legacy asset-reconstruction manifests from being fully allocated. Each uses its existing 16 MiB contract. Regression definitions cover exact/over-limit payloads and invalid bounds. Dataset payloads keep their separate handling; corpus completeness and validation remain open.

**Edition 3.537 G5.14 positioning SRS Hyper-SFN timing gate (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Periodic positioning-SRS counter resolution now applies the TS 38.211 V19.2.0 clause 6.4.1.4.4 parity condition `(n_HFN + N_SRS_HFN) mod 2 = 0` when configured period `T_SRS >= 2^mu * 10240` slots and validates HFN in the 0–1023 range. The per-resource frequency-allocation API and exported periodic/semi-persistent schedule composition API pass the already-decoded positioning flag, numerology, Hyper-SFN number, and index through to the counter. Regression definitions cover eligible/ineligible parity, allocation/schedule integration, invalid short periods, HFN bounds, and non-positioning use. Full ASN.1, resource-set scheduling, collision handling, and positioning reports remain open; no validation or qualification is claimed. [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.523-3 V18.5.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138500_138599/13852303/18.05.00_60/ts_13852303v180500p.pdf).

**Edition 3.531 G7.04 trajectory target support accounting (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `NRTrajectory.target_support_counts()` now reports record presence, missing-target records, total/selected/masked element counts, and records whose absent mask implicitly selects all values. Mask broadcasting follows each record's target shape and works with NumPy and optional PyTorch tensors. Regression definitions cover partial masks, absent targets, and implicit masks; this does not replace a release-wide per-split/per-family report.

**Edition 3.530 G7.03 NR target finite-value boundary (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** `NRChannelRecord` now rejects empty target names/shapes, non-numeric target arrays, and NaN/Inf values before records can enter storage or learning. The check supports NumPy arrays and optional PyTorch tensors without copying GPU targets to CPU. Regression definitions cover these invalid values; validation is deferred.

**Edition 3.529 G7.15 profile-proxy dataset semantic guard (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Profile-disjoint training now requires the dataset-level label declaration to be `profile_proxy` in addition to its existing per-row PROFILE_PROXY semantics and A/B/C-versus-D/E label check. This prevents a profile-proxy corpus from being scored under a conflicting dataset-level meaning such as geometric LOS. A regression definition covers that mismatch; no validation has run.

**Edition 3.528 G7.08 signed child artifact semantic row coverage (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the signed per-kind child artifact audit to require ChannelDataset NPZ artifacts and verify each row's child ID, child kind, and parent subject against the frozen registry binding. The auditor extracts only the bounded `metadata_json.npy` member after streamed byte/hash verification and emits row counts and metadata digests; `children_audited` is true only after exact child roster and row-semantic checks pass. Regression definitions cover valid rows and wrong child/parent bindings. The audit remains input-scoped and does not establish complete-corpus authority, acquisition provenance, or release qualification; no checks have run.

**Edition 3.527 G5.14 DCI 0_3/1_3 decoded SRS request-list resolution (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported resolver for a decoded request-list index and per-cell request row. It applies the per-cell two-bit typeB SRS request in ascending serving-cell order, including the optional leading SUL selector and carrier-scoped resource-set matching. Regression definitions cover multi-cell trigger selection, single-cell SUL routing, and malformed indexes/rosters. This does not decode DCI bits or RRC ASN.1, resolve multi-cell scheduled-cell combinations or SRS offsets, implement typeA, or qualify G5.14. No checks have run. Mapping follows [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Table 7.3.1.1.2-24 and DCI 0_3/1_3 SRS request-list definitions](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.526 G5.14 configured SRS request modes and supplementary-uplink routing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The typeB resolver requires the configured `srs-RequestDCI-*` mode for DCI 0_2/1_2 (one- or two-bit request field) and retains the two-bit request field for DCI 0_1/1_1. Optional supplementary-uplink configuration adds a leading non-SUL/SUL carrier selector, and selected resource sets are scoped using explicit carrier metadata. The schedule-composition API propagates these parameters. Regression definitions cover the configured modes, field bounds, missing configuration, and carrier routing; no checks have run. DCI 0_3/1_3, typeA, full ASN.1 decoding, and receiver conformance remain open. [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Tables 7.3.1.1.1-1, 7.3.1.1.2-24, and 7.3.1.1.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.508 G7 exact episode-manifest path coverage (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The corpus audit now accepts only canonical POSIX manifest paths beneath `manifests/episodes/` and compares their full relative-path set to the discovered manifest set. This closes a basename-alias loophole where a copied manifest outside the canonical directory could mask an unlisted canonical entry. A regression definition covers the alias; no checks have run, and corpus qualification remains open.

**Edition 3.507 G4 dual-end directional CDL pattern evidence (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a frozen 40-case CDL-A–E regression family with directional element patterns and separate 3D orientations at both link ends. The independent table reference covers 776 per-cluster claims under one simultaneous 99% interval family and a fixed precision cap; a contiguous fresh seed roster requires collision audit before execution. The regression and protocol are authored, not run. This checks bounded standardized-model consistency only and does not close G4 or the RT gate.

**Edition 3.506 G3 run-receipt source binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The standalone joint-evidence run manifest now binds the complete fixed roster of evaluator/scorer modules, the bounded-file helper, and the input/artifact/manifest schemas, with sizes, SHA-256 values, and an aggregate dependency-bundle digest alongside the exact run files. A builder and CLI create the receipt; verification rejects missing/extra roster entries or source drift. The receipt cannot attest loaded Python code objects, third-party binaries, acquisition provenance, external review, or scientific qualification. Regression definitions cover source drift. Validation remains deferred; G3 and production D1 gates remain closed.

**Edition 3.505 G3 joint evidence run manifest (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a versioned standalone manifest that binds the five-branch joint evaluator input and output to exact relative paths, roles, lengths, and SHA-256 digests. Its verifier reads bounded inventoried bytes without following symlinks and deterministically replays the captured input bytes. Regression definitions cover successful five-branch replay and digest tampering. This is deliberately separate from the material-only G3.03 v0.3 proposal package; it does not provide acquisition crosswalks, evaluator source identity, external attestation, or qualification. Validation remains deferred and G3/D1 remain gated.

**Edition 3.504 G3 five-branch file-bound evaluator artifact (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a strict file adapter and CLI for the existing acceptance-family-v4 numerical bundle, including canonical JSON input/output, `$complex` scalar encoding, bounded no-symlink/single-link stable reads, exact input-byte digest, serialized bundle digest, no-overwrite report creation, and deterministic replay. Versioned input/artifact schemas and a procedure document define the serialized contract. Artifacts remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`; this is not a G3.03 v0.4 acquisition package, does not bind records to authenticated acquisition inventory entries, does not authenticate measurement provenance or evaluator code identity, and does not change G3 or D1 gates. Regression definitions remain unrun.

**Edition 3.503 G5.14 selected SRS resource-set scheduling composition (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported end-to-end composition for the bounded two-bit typeB DCI request path: resolve all matching configured aperiodic resource-set IDs, apply each selected set's target-slot offset configuration, and compute its per-symbol type-1 frequency-hop allocations. Only selected sets require allocation records; malformed set IDs, triggers, and selected allocation fields fail closed. Regression definitions cover multi-set trigger resolution through target slot and hop roster, no-trigger, and missing selected-set allocation. This does not parse full RRC/DCI messages, support typeA or DCI 0_3/1_3, check symbol-level timing feasibility, derive full multiport schedules, or qualify G5.14. No checks have run. Trigger mapping follows [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Table 7.3.1.1.2-24](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.502 G5.14 SRS request-to-resource-set resolution (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported resolver for SRS request values in DCI formats 0_1/0_2/1_1/1_2 when `srs-TPC-PDCCH-Group=typeB`. The format-specific request widths are recorded in edition 3.524 below. TypeA and DCI 0_3/1_3 remain outside this single-cell typeB resolver because their antenna-switching and multi-cell request-index semantics are not implemented here. Regression definitions cover exact set selection, inactive-resource exclusion, no-trigger behavior, duplicate IDs, and unsupported context. No checks have run; full RRC decoding, complete DCI support, end-to-end receiver conformance, and G5.14 qualification remain open. Source: [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Table 7.3.1.1.2-24](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.524 G5.14 DCI-specific SRS request width (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected typeB SRS-trigger resolution to honor the one-bit request in DCI formats 0_2/1_2 (0 means no set; 1 selects trigger 1), while retaining the two-bit request in formats 0_1/1_1. Codepoints 2/3 are now rejected for 0_2/1_2. Regression definitions cover both formats' valid and invalid widths. This follows TS 38.212 V19.2.0 Table 7.3.1.1.3-1 and does not implement the separate multi-cell 0_3/1_3 request-list mapping or typeA antenna-switching mode. [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.2.0, Table 7.3.1.1.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.02.00_60/ts_138212v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.501 G5.14 aperiodic SRS schedule composition (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported typed adapter that binds caller-selected aperiodic target-slot timing to each resource-symbol type-1 frequency allocation and hop counter. The caller must resolve DCI-to-resource-set selection and decoded RRC values; this is not complete SRS scheduling or end-to-end conformance. Regression definitions cover selected available-slot-offset mapping, target-slot identity, per-symbol hops, and missing-index rejection. No checks have run; G5.14 and production D1 gates remain open.

**Edition 3.500 G7 split-policy child-kind roster audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added and publicly exported `audit_split_policy_child_roster` to verify exact coverage of one descendant kind within a frozen registry containing potentially several child kinds. The UMCD corpus auditor now records one digest-bound report per observed kind. Reports bind registry, sorted child-ID, exact binding, and audit digests; malformed, mixed, duplicate, missing, extra, or changed bindings fail closed. Regression definitions remain unrun. The API is input-scoped and does not replace complete saved-corpus inventory or signed release review.

**Edition 3.499 G5.14 SRS per-resource frequency schedule assembly (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a composition API that resolves per-symbol `n-SRS` values for an already-resolved aperiodic/periodic/semi-persistent occasion and passes them into the standardized type-1 frequency-tree allocation. It returns all symbol allocations for active occasions and `None` when a periodic resource is inactive. Regression definitions cover sequential occasions and per-symbol frequency positions. It does not derive `T-SRS`/`T-offset` from RRC or implement resource-set triggers; no tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.498 G5.14 Release 19 SRS cyclic-shift hopping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added the TS 38.211 V19.2.0 `f_csh` sequence-index resolver, with a 128-frame period, numerology-dependent slots per frame, symbol-level coordinates, optional higher-layer hopping-subset bitmap, and finer-granularity shifts. Integrated the per-symbol offset into single- and multiport type-1 sequence generation. Regression definitions cover frame periodicity, subset selection constraints, symbol bounds, and fractional-shift phase. No checks have run; ASN.1 schedule decoding and end-to-end SRS conformance remain open.

**Edition 3.497 G5.14 SRS antenna-port cyclic-shift derivation (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added TS 38.211 V19.2.0 antenna-port cyclic-shift index derivation from the configured SRS port roster, transmission-comb shift count, and base cyclic shift. The implementation covers the eight-port TDM effective four-port remapping and special comb/port-count cases. Regression definitions cover supported rosters and malformed configurations; they remain unrun. Cyclic-shift hopping and higher-layer resource scheduling remain open.

**Edition 3.496 G5.14 SRS multiport transmit-power normalization (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Updated ordinary, positioning, and frequency-hopped multiport mappers to apply the TS 38.211 `1/sqrt(N_ap)` sequence amplitude factor before resource-grid placement. The four-port three-transmit option uses the specified effective port count of three and rejects use with any port count other than four. Regression definitions update two-port β scaling and cover the four-port special case and its invalid configuration. Caller-supplied port sequences remain caller-scheduled; no standards-derived orthogonal schedule is claimed. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.495 G5.14 caller-scheduled multiport hopped SRS mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the per-symbol frequency-hop mapper to `[ports, SRS symbols, occupied REs]` inputs and added immutable per-port result grids that retain the shared hop-counter, position-index, positioning-offset, and RE rosters. This maps caller-supplied port sequences and does not derive standardized port schedules or orthogonalization. Regression definitions cover two ports across distinct frequency hops with positioning offsets, exact RE placement, masks, and read-only outputs. Higher-layer schedule resolution, standards-derived port scheduling, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.494 G5.14 per-symbol SRS frequency-hop grid mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a one-port resource mapper that accepts a resolved frequency allocation per SRS symbol, preserves each allocation's `n-SRS`/frequency-tree roster, and supports the positioning-resource comb offset on top of the hop position. A single allocation may also be broadcast across the resource symbols for fixed-position mapping. Regression definitions cover distinct hop positions across symbols and the combined hopping/positioning path. The mapper consumes caller-resolved allocations; standards-driven higher-layer scheduling, multiport hopped mapping, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.788 G5.14 bounded SRS hopping verification (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Verified the existing type-1 frequency-tree recurrence and scheduled per-symbol `n-SRS` integration against ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0 clause 6.4.1.4.3, including explicit-counter enforcement, odd/even tree radices, periodic/aperiodic counter behavior, and single-/multiport hopped grid mapping. Thirteen selected SRS regressions pass with Ruff clean. This supersedes earlier “not implemented” and “unrun” statements for the bounded software component only; decoded higher-layer schedule completeness, raw ASN.1/PER and CRC/polar processing, independent full conformance, and receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.789 G5 retained-PUSCH-payload mutation guard (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Record admission now stores the sidecar shape as an immutable tuple, and `write_nr_record` rechecks bit values, size, shape, and SHA-256 immediately before publication. This prevents a caller from re-enabling NumPy array writes or mutating nested shape provenance to publish a self-inconsistent NR object. The isolated NR record/store suites pass 79 tests; changed Python files pass Ruff and compilation, and `git diff --check` passes. Native transmitter-to-decoder execution and G5 receiver qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.790 bounded City43 zero-scattering LOS Phase-0 diagnosis (2026-10-27, existing diagnostics only):** The retained depth-10, seed-23, 100k/1M SISO comparison is dominated by a zero-interaction aggregate: valid zero-order records increase 9→75, collapse to one coefficient/delay/geometry fingerprint, and explain 0.999949 of total CFR-delta norm under separate coherent aggregation. Matched nonzero paths have sub-millimeter vertex and sub-0.0001 ns delay changes; their maximum coefficient relative error is 7.9e-5. Interaction-order counts grow with budget, including 15→105 order-10 paths, but reported order-10 coherent power remains far below the zero-interaction term. The bounded interpretation is budget-dependent discovery/multiplicity of repeated zero-interaction records followed by coherent accumulation. The reports do not explain why the solver returns those records, qualify other configurations, or make 1M ground truth. No new RT run was made. Full evidence and source digests are in `docs/research/CITY43_ZERO_SCATTERING_PHASE0_DIAGNOSIS_2026-10-27.md`; G3 and production RT remain gated.

**Edition 3.493 G5.14 SRS transmission-counter resolution from scheduled occasion data (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added TS 38.211 `n-SRS` counter calculation for aperiodic and periodic/semi-persistent resources from already-resolved resource-symbol index, repetition factor, eight-port TDM mode, absolute slot, periodicity, and slot offset. Off-schedule periodic slots return no transmission. Regression definitions cover repetition groups, eight-port TDM, occasion increments, and off-schedule handling. Resolving `T-SRS`/`T-offset` from RRC configuration and full resource/set scheduling remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.492 G5.14 type-1 SRS frequency hopping for explicit transmission counters (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Implemented the TS 38.211 frequency-tree index recurrence for `b-hop < b-SRS`, including the specified unit radix at the hopping level and even/odd bandwidth-count cases. The fixed-allocation resolver now accepts caller-supplied `n-SRS` and derives the allocation for that transmission; it fails closed when active hopping lacks the counter. Regression definitions include a hand-enumerated eight-counter sequence from a standardized table row, allocation binding, required-counter behavior, and existing no-hop table-tree cases. Counter derivation from periodic/semi-persistent schedules, aperiodic resource scheduling, and higher-layer configuration remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.491 G5.14 caller-scheduled multiport positioning SRS mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an exported `[ports, SRS symbols, occupied REs]` mapper for `SRS-PosResource` that reuses the per-symbol positioning-offset implementation and returns per-port grids, masks, symbol-wise RE rosters, and offsets. It accepts caller-provided port sequences and therefore does not claim standards-derived port scheduling or orthogonalization. Regression definitions cover two distinct ports across four positioning symbols and mask/index integrity. No tests or static checks have run. Active frequency hopping, higher-layer resource/set scheduling, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.490 G5.14 positioning SRS per-symbol resource-grid mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a single-port `SRS-PosResource` mapper that applies the TS 38.211 Table 6.4.1.4.3-2 offset independently at each resource symbol and records each symbol's actual subcarrier roster. The fixed frequency origin and bandwidth are caller-resolved; ordinary `SRS-Resource` mapping remains on the zero-offset path. Regression definitions cover four-symbol placement, per-symbol sequence rows, one-row broadcast with beta scaling, exact masks and RE indices, immutable views, and invalid configurations. A malformed duplicate-keyword regression was corrected; no tests or static checks have run. Multiport positioning resources, active frequency hopping, higher-layer resource/set scheduling, independent conformance, and receiver qualification remain open. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3 and Table 6.4.1.4.3-2](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.489 G5.14 fixed-position SRS frequency-tree and positioning-offset resolution (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a TS 38.211 type-1 no-frequency-hopping resolver that computes every `n_b` from the standardized `c-SRS`/`b-SRS` table and `freqDomainPosition`, `freqDomainShift`, and comb offset, returning a validated allocation for the existing mapper. Added all supported `SRS-PosResource` per-symbol offsets from Table 6.4.1.4.3-2; ordinary `SRS-Resource` correctly uses zero. Active frequency hopping fails closed because its counter/tree sequence is not implemented yet. Regression definitions cover every supported positioning-table row, a multi-level allocation and mapped RE bounds, invalid inputs, and active-hopping rejection. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3 and Tables 6.4.1.4.3-1/2](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).

**Edition 3.488 G3 joint numerical-evaluator orchestration (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added an in-memory runner and recomputation verifier that binds the material-calibration, complex-CSI, propagation-metric, receiver-impact, and diagnostic path-equivalence scorers to one validated acceptance-family-v4 digest. Per-branch input/output hashes make the numerical reports reproducible and detect bundle tampering; all output remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`, and the runner explicitly cannot prove external measurement truth, provenance, independent units, preregistration, representativeness, reviewer authority, or rights. Regression definitions cover five-branch recomputation, common-family binding, tampering, input-byte sensitivity, malformed/mixed inputs, v3-family rejection, and nonfinite evidence. No checks have run; G3 acceptance criteria and evidence gates are unchanged.

**Edition 3.487 G5.14 complete SRS bandwidth table lookup (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Expanded the fixed-bandwidth resolver to all 64 `c-SRS` rows and all four `b-SRS` levels (`m_SRS,b` and `N_b` from TS 38.211 Table 6.4.1.4.3-1). The returned sequence length and occupied-subcarrier extent follow the selected row and transmission comb. Regression definitions retain all 64 `b-SRS=0` values and check representative multi-subband entries through `b-SRS=3`, invalid levels, and consistency of the public value object. Frequency-position/hopping derivation and resource scheduling remain open; no tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, Table 6.4.1.4.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.487 G5.14 complete SRS bandwidth table lookup (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Expanded the fixed-bandwidth resolver to all 64 `c-SRS` rows and all four `b-SRS` levels (`m_SRS,b` and `N_b` from TS 38.211 Table 6.4.1.4.3-1). The returned sequence length and occupied-subcarrier extent follow the selected row and transmission comb. Regression definitions retain all 64 `b-SRS=0` values and check representative multi-subband entries through `b-SRS=3`, invalid levels, and consistency of the public value object. Frequency-position/hopping derivation and resource scheduling remain open; no tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, Table 6.4.1.4.3-1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.486 G5.14 fixed-bandwidth SRS allocation extent (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added all 64 standardized `c-SRS` `m_SRS,0` bandwidth entries for `b-SRS=0`, with comb-derived sequence lengths, and bounded single-/multiport mapping to an allocated bandwidth inside a wider carrier grid. Regression definitions cover every table row for combs 2/4/8, invalid configuration values, and no-write-outside-allocation placement. Frequency-position derivation, `b-SRS>0`, frequency hopping, complete higher-layer scheduling, and standards/receiver qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, Table 6.4.1.4.3-1 and clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.485 G5.14 caller-scheduled multiport SRS code path (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added explicit cyclic-shift sequence generation per transmit port and a `[ports, symbols, subcarriers]` grid mapper with immutable masks. Regression definitions cover distinct-shift orthogonality for a 72-element sequence, multiport mapping, invalid/duplicate shift schedules, shape errors, and mask integrity. This implements caller-specified shifts; deriving standards-valid port schedules and frequency allocations from higher-layer configuration, broader SRS conformance, and receiver-impact qualification remain open. No tests or static checks have run. Normative sequence basis: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clauses 5.2.2 and 6.4.1.4](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.468 G5.14 bounded SRS low-PAPR sequence generation (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added TS 38.211 type-1 low-PAPR sequence generation for explicit sequence lengths ≥36, group indices, base-sequence indices, and cyclic-shift indices, including the prime-length root construction. Short-sequence tables, derivation of valid SRS parameters from higher-layer configuration, sequence/group hopping, per-port cyclic-shift scheduling, and full SRS conformance remain open. Independent scalar-formula regression definitions are authored but unrun. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 5.2.2.1 and 6.4.1.4.2.

**Edition 3.467 bounded G5.14 SRS resource-grid mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a one-port, one-hop mapper for caller-supplied complex SRS sequence rows. It applies the slot-end-relative SRS symbol start, transmission comb sizes 2/4/8, comb offset, sequence-to-RE placement, and an explicit resource mask. The implementation follows the bounded mapping contract in [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 6.4.1.4.1–6.4.1.4.3. Sequence generation, c-SRS/b-SRS allocation derivation, hopping, multiport orthogonalization, scheduling, and independent standards validation remain open; regression definitions are authored but unrun.

**Edition 3.466 G7.09 split parent/child ID collision guard (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Frozen split registries now reject a child artifact ID that collides with any statistical parent ID. This prevents consumers that resolve parent IDs before child IDs from silently assigning the parent interpretation to a child and bypassing its explicit inheritance binding. A regression definition covers the ambiguous-ID case; validation is pending and no corpus split is qualified.

**Edition 3.465 G7.07 legacy CFR input firewall (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `ChannelDataset.model_inputs()` returning a read-only CFR-only view, and routed legacy experiment feature construction through it. The view exposes no labels, grouping, domain/source IDs, metadata, or generation report. Regression definitions assert field isolation and read-only behavior. This formalizes the current classifier input boundary but does not audit every downstream adapter or prove corpus-wide leakage absence; no tests or static checks have run.

**Edition 3.464 G5.09 receive-time path activity envelopes (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The sampled multipath operator now accepts finite real per-path activity envelopes in `[0,1]` over receive samples, allowing callers to express path appearance, disappearance, and fading while retaining the existing constant-coefficient behavior by default. Regression definitions cover active/inactive transitions with nonzero delay and malformed envelopes. This is a bounded synthetic mechanism, not a validated path birth/death law or deployment channel model; no tests or static checks have run, and G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.463 G3 matched path-class diagnostic integrity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The roster evaluator now rejects matched class records with missing, negative, boolean, or non-finite vertex, delay, direction, or aggregate complex-CFR deltas. These values remain diagnostic because the frozen criteria do not define a separate per-class margin; CSI and delay/angular population limits are evaluated by their dedicated G3 scorers. Regression definitions cover malformed matched-class deltas. No tests or static checks have run; G3 qualification and production RT remain gated.

**Edition 3.462 G4.07 TDL-A–E receive-covariance family authored (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added five CPU-native cases under one simultaneous 99% family covering every real/imaginary entry of a configured complex 4×4 receiver covariance matrix across scalable TDL-A–E. TDL-D/E use a diffuse tap to keep the estimand separate from the specular LOS atom. These are regression definitions only; they have not run and do not establish full fading or receiver conformance.

**Edition 3.461 G5.09 NPY metadata preflight (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The retained-evidence auditor now inspects ZIP roster/expanded sizes and each NPY header before `np.load`, rejecting unsupported versions/dtypes and shape-to-payload mismatches. This catches forged allocation metadata before array materialization while preserving the compressed and uncompressed caps. A forged-shape regression is authored; no tests or audit have run.

**Edition 3.460 G5.09 avoid duplicate decompressed waveform arrays (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The auditor now retains the arrays materialized by `NpzFile` after archive-roster and aggregate uncompressed-size checks instead of copying them a second time. This reduces the evidence-loading peak without changing the accepted 512-MiB compressed/1-GiB uncompressed contract; bounded memory is not a receiver qualification result.

**Edition 3.459 G3 material angular-array strict parsing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the shared G3 real-number parser to shaped arrays and applied it to measured/predicted material angular-bin power and solid-angle matrices. Coercible booleans, strings, complex values, and non-finite cells now fail before scoring. Regression definitions cover each matrix field; this does not establish measurement validity or qualify G3.

**Edition 3.458 G3 shared strict real-number parsing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a shared scalar/vector parser and applied it to receiver-impact scoring and scenario assurance. Their SNR grids, comparison SNR, observed record SNRs, and scenario probabilities reject boolean/string/complex/non-finite coercions. Regression definitions cover each boundary. This improves evidence-input integrity only; qualification and production RT remain gated.

**Edition 3.457 G3 receiver scorer strict SNR inputs (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The paired NR receiver-impact scorer now uses the shared strict real-number parser for its SNR grid, comparison point, and per-outcome SNR. Strings, booleans, complex values, and non-finite numbers cannot be coerced into scoring inputs. Regression definitions cover malformed grid and comparison inputs. This is evidence-input integrity only; no receiver qualification or production RT authorization follows.

**Edition 3.456 G3 receiver-assurance strict numeric inputs (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The evaluator-aligned scenario planner now rejects coercible strings, booleans, complex values, and non-finite values in SNR grids and IID scenario probabilities. Regression definitions cover those invalid assumptions. The planner remains hypothetical and non-admitting; no complete G3 sample size, qualification, or production RT authorization follows.

**Edition 3.455 G5.09 bounded waveform-evidence member metadata (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Bounded the streaming NPZ writer's member count and identifier length/character set, limiting archive-header overhead under the existing uncompressed-size allowance. Regression definitions cover unsafe/path-like/non-ASCII/overlong names and excess members. Tests and receiver runs remain deferred; no G5 qualification or production RT authorization follows.

**Edition 3.454 G5.09 streaming waveform-evidence budget regressions (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added regression definitions for both rejection boundaries: cumulative raw NPY payload above the uncompressed cap and ZIP output crossing the compressed high-water cap. The round-trip regression checks exact array preservation. These are authored tests only; no validation or receiver run has occurred, and G5.09/production RT remain gated.
**Edition 3.453 G5.09 streaming waveform-evidence archive (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Replaced full-array stacking with per-channel NPY streaming into compressed NPZ members. The writer checks consistent shapes/dtypes and total uncompressed size before emission and retains the compressed-output cap. Regression source checks exact array round-trip; validation and receiver runs remain deferred, and no G5.09 qualification follows.
**Edition 3.452 G5.09 sidecar destination safety (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Waveform evidence output now accepts only sibling-safe `.npz` filenames, refuses existing destinations before receiver initialization, and retains atomic no-overwrite publication. The auditor enforces the same manifest filename rule. Regression source checks early rejection and preserves existing bytes; this does not qualify G5.09.
**Edition 3.451 G5.09 waveform-evidence manifest contract (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a versioned JSON Schema for the sibling NPZ manifest, including safe filename form, digest, byte/channel bounds, and the exact evidence-array roster. Regression source covers valid and malformed metadata and the receiver-generated form. No new receiver evidence was generated; this does not close G5.09.
**Edition 3.450 G5.09 bounded waveform-evidence writer (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The receiver runner enforces the auditor's 1-GiB uncompressed and 512-MiB compressed limits while retaining raw evidence. It checks per-channel tensor bytes before retaining them and caps compressed writes by high-water mark. The guard regression is authored but unrun; this does not qualify the receiver or authorize production RT.
**Edition 3.449 G4.07 structured seed-collision audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a bounded scanner over saved `.json`/`.json.gz` artifacts for preregistered integer seeds in seed-named fields, inclusive seed ranges, and seed-bearing result keys. It records requested roots and eligible-file counts, per-file hashes, and exact match paths; rejects duplicate keys, linked/hard-linked or changing inputs, and size-limit violations; treats numeric string/float matches as possible collisions and malformed seed fields/ranges as unresolved. The two CDL Fourier protocols prescribe exact seed ranges and minimum corpus roots. The collision scan remains unrun and mandatory before either confirmatory family; no G4 qualification is claimed.
**Edition 3.448 G3 fail-closed path-record parsing (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Path geometry, complex CFR components, serialized class delays/signatures/directions, and record object types are now checked without coercing booleans, strings, complex values, or non-finite values into numeric evidence. This is an integrity improvement to diagnostic path comparisons; it does not qualify path truth or authorize production RT.
**Current implementation update (2026-10-25):** G3 joint-family v4 binds the path-equivalence family/LOS-NLOS/independent-episode roster and the two-direction 95% per-episode coverage threshold. Episodes are checked separately without pooling and remain diagnostic-only; they do not increase the inferential Bonferroni claim count. The roster reconciles reported coverage with matched/relevant class counts. A bounded JSON CLI and input schema evaluate retained records against the hash-valid v4 family; run it through `loslab g3-path-roster-audit <input.json>`. A versioned output schema describes the diagnostic report. The CLI rejects duplicate-key/non-finite JSON and linked, oversized, or changing input files. Acquisition planning and data contracts accept v4 alongside v3. Implementation remains unvalidated; production RT generation remains gated.
**Edition 3.447 G4 spherical angular-power primitives (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added reusable full-sphere binning with exact solid-angle weights, normalized integrated power and per-steradian density, explicit pole/azimuth-wrap rules, and a global-to-element-local direction transform using the pinned Euler convention. Finite-support cluster atoms carry paired transport-block CRC outcomes across the frozen SNR grid; each simulated dataset goes through the receiver evaluator. Exact binomial Monte Carlo lower bounds are computed per LOS/NLOS stratum and combined with a union bound, without assuming cross-stratum independence. The v4 input schema and CLI report receiver assurance separately from material-unit and CSI/propagation cluster designs. The v0.3 package verifier accepts and digest-checks the v3 family while retaining proposal-only status. Ten focused G3 modules plus Ruff, format, targeted mypy, schema validation, and whitespace checks pass. This is hypothetical design tooling: no receiver is qualified, no complete G3 sample size is reported, and production D1 remains gated.
**Edition 3.401 G3 shared acceptance family v3 includes receiver-impact intervals (2026-10-24):** The cross-evaluator family now counts four receiver claims per preregistered family/LOS-NLOS stratum and supplies the same multiplicity-adjusted alpha to the paired NR receiver scorer. Material, complex-CSI, propagation, and receiver reports bind to the same family hash. The planner fails closed on v3 until receiver assurance uses a validated design matching its hierarchical cluster/block bootstrap; therefore the complete G3 design remains open and production D1 is still gated.

**Edition 3.399 G3 shared acceptance family v2 (2026-10-24):** Introduced a versioned family that jointly counts material-calibration, complex-CSI, and delay/angular inferential claims. The six-module G3 regression slice and changed-code checks pass. **Edition 3.398 G3 delay/angular metric multiplicity correction (2026-10-24):** The numerical propagation scorer now controls one Bonferroni 95% family over p95-bootstrap and cluster-failure bounds for all four delay/angular metrics across every preregistered family/LOS-NLOS/component stratum. The full declared roster is counted even where metrics are unresolved or not applicable. One stratum therefore has eight claims and requires 100 independent zero-failure clusters to pass the adjusted 5% upper bound; 59 do not. Two strata use 16 claims. All 11 focused tests, Ruff, format, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks pass. This remains a local scorer family: integration with material, complex-CSI, and NR-impact inference plus complete G3 sample-size assurance remains open.

At variance 0.001/0.003, the static reference equal-channel BLER is 0.047/0.031, while continuous 750-Hz echo BLER is 0.641/0.570; the corresponding continuous-minus-symbol-static differences are −0.023/−0.016.
