**Edition 3.362 G5.14 per-chain-noise PBCH MRC (2026-10-24):** PBCH receive and blind acquisition now accept a shared noise variance or one complex-sample variance per receive chain. Unequal-noise, unequal-gain, frequency-selective known-occasion tests confirm combined soft information equals the sum of single-branch results; blind two-chain acquisition also recovers the SSB with unequal branch noise. Invalid per-chain variance values are rejected. Focused tests and changed-path Ruff/format/mypy/whitespace checks pass. This remains synthetic static-channel evidence; broad receiver/field qualification and production D1 remain gated.

The D/E specular LOS atoms are excluded from this diffuse coupling statistic and remain covered only by separate LOS tests. All five profiles pass with Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks. **Edition 3.350 G5.09 independent audit of shared-sync confirmation (2026-10-24):** Added a standalone auditor for the one-time 64-channel × 8-block result. It independently recomputes retained timing/CFO arrays, BLER summaries, paired channel bootstrap, CFO channel-tail exact bounds, and the frozen acceptance verdict, while checking result/config/protocol/source hashes. All retained-data checks pass; the report's experiment source bytes match the current worktree, but the report records that they were uncommitted at run time. Once source is included in the research implementation commit, run the auditor against that commit's blob; do not rerun the frozen seed set. Samplewise reference residuals and wrong-order waveforms were not retained, so those two reported scalar measurements cannot be independently recomputed from this artifact. The result remains conditional synthetic evidence; broad G5 qualification and RT production remain open.
**Edition 3.349 G4 CDL-D/E diffuse ray-table geometry (2026-10-24):** Added direct native Sionna 2.2 comparisons for diffuse rays in the LOS CDL-D/E profiles. The test binds the v19.2 table hashes, excludes the first profile row because it is the separately composed specular LOS path, and checks the normalized powers and all 20 ray angles for each remaining diffuse cluster. All 17 cases in the focused delay/angular test file pass. This does not verify the composed LOS field or stochastic angular-power confidence and does not close G4.07, G3, or authorize RT production.
**Edition 3.348 G3 complex-CSI bootstrap assurance (2026-10-24):** The v3 acquisition-design schema/CLI now adds explicitly hypothetical scene-cluster summaries and evaluator-aligned CSI assurance: equal-cluster median/p95 criteria, the family-alpha Bonferroni bootstrap upper bound, and the exact cluster-failure bound. Scenarios must cover every shared joint-family LOS/NLOS stratum; counts remain separate from material acquisition units and no combined sample size is inferred. Focused planner/CLI tests and targeted static checks pass. This is planning software only; externally justified assumptions, preregistration, representative independent evidence, G3 qualification, and production RT authorization remain open.
**Edition 3.347 G4.07 CDL ray-angle distribution geometry (2026-10-24):** Native Sionna 2.2 ray outputs for CDL-A/B/C now match the hash-pinned v19.2 table's normalized cluster weights and all 20 ray angles/cluster across AoA, AoD, ZoA, and ZoD within 1e-4 degrees. The full focused file passes 15 tests; Ruff, formatting, mypy, and whitespace checks pass. The check validates deterministic profile ray construction only, not stochastic power-weighted angular-distribution confidence; LOS D/E, non-omni patterns, general arrays, and RT/material truth remain open.
**Edition 3.346 G5.09 shared CP synchronization confirmation (2026-10-24):** The frozen shared-acquisition protocol was run once on its disjoint 64-channel seed set (991001–991064), eight blocks per channel. Timing error is zero, CFO P95/max is 103.32/131.38 Hz, clean/corrected BLER is 0/6.64%, reference error is 1.02e-6, and wrong-order RMSE is 0.00169; all frozen synthetic-fixture limits pass. No channel cluster had a block at/above either 250- or 350-Hz CFO threshold (exact one-sided 95% upper bound 0.04573). Corrected-minus-clean BLER's paired channel-bootstrap interval is [0.0469, 0.0879]. The source-hash-bound artifact verifies, and the new retained-data audit reproduces its derivable summaries. Its source was uncommitted at run time, so verify the exact artifact source hash against the later research commit; do not rerun these frozen seeds. It qualifies only this synthetic fixture; G5 remains open for general receiver/physical evidence and production RT remains gated.
**Edition 3.345 G5.09 shared CP synchronization protocol preregistration (2026-10-24):** Froze one timing/CFO estimate per independent channel cluster by concatenating its eight repeated waveforms on OFDM-symbol boundaries, with a fixed 2-sample timing guard and 6-sample family-maximum CFO guard. One acquired correction is applied to every block; the estimator sees no channel path truth. Existing acceptance limits remain unchanged. The sole confirmation is seeds 991001–991064 with eight blocks/channel and a source-hash-bound saved result; do not retune from it. On disjoint development seeds 990001–990064, point criteria passed (timing 0, CFO P95/max 91.10/110.08 Hz, corrected BLER 9.96%), but the paired channel-bootstrap interval was [0.0664, 0.1367], so this is not confirmatory evidence and its upper bound exceeds the 10% point limit. The confirmation was subsequently run and is recorded in edition 3.346; it remains synthetic-only, and D1 production generation stays gated.
**Edition 3.344 G5.09 channel-cluster CFO tail audit (2026-10-24):** The ordered-impairment receiver report now supplements block-pooled CFO P95/max with channel-cluster exceedance counts and exact one-sided 95% upper bounds at the unchanged 250-Hz and 350-Hz limits. In the existing failed fixed-guard diagnostic, 26/64 channels have at least one block exceeding 250 Hz and 7/64 exceed 350 Hz; respective upper bounds are 0.517 and 0.196. This conservative statistic is conditional on blocks per channel and does not establish acceptance; old samples remain diagnostic-only. The refreshed report schema and focused test pass with Ruff, format, and targeted mypy. G5.09 still requires a frozen synchronization method and a fresh independent confirmation; production RT remains gated.
**Edition 3.343 joint G3 assumption-driven sample planning (2026-10-24):** Acquisition-design CLI/schema v2 now consumes the hash-bound joint G3 family, allocates confidence and planned power across the entire claim roster, and reports separate hypothetical counts for lab material units and CSI scene clusters. The material bootstrap-assurance calculation also uses the global family claim count. The planner deliberately returns no combined sample size; assumptions are unverified, the bootstrap does not provide joint CSI assurance, and no measurement or qualification evidence is created. Focused CLI/planner tests pass.
**Edition 3.342 shared G3 acceptance-family accounting:** A hash-bound roster now enumerates the material and complex-CSI interval claims together; both numerical scorers consume the same roster and use its full claim count for Bonferroni adjustment. Five material interval claims per material stratum and two CSI upper-bound claims per CSI stratum are explicit. Local scorer families remain diagnostic-only when no joint roster is supplied. Focused tests and static checks pass. The roster has not been independently preregistered, and the acquisition-design planner has not yet derived a precision-powered sample size from this combined count; external verification and qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.341 split-lock signature verification tests (2026-10-24):** Verified the Release Split Policy v2 OpenSSH signature boundary using an ephemeral test signer and a separate temporary `allowed_signers` keyring. All 53 focused split-policy tests, Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. Modified bytes, an untrusted principal, and a hard-linked signature are rejected. This is implementation evidence only: no institutional trust key, signed production registry, corpus-wide lineage audit, or release approval is present.
**Edition 3.340 G3.03 full-sphere angular input contract (2026-10-24):** Added evaluator-input v2 and acquisition proposal v0.3 for 2D zenith/azimuth integrated-power grids, explicit coordinate frames, verified spherical cell solid angles, and missing-bin rejection. Legacy package v0.2 and evaluator-input v1 remain supported as proposal compatibility. Focused evaluator/package/CLI tests, Ruff, formatting, targeted mypy, and whitespace checks pass. The normalized 2D CDF score is a project-defined diagnostic; no material data were acquired, G3 remains unqualified, cross-evaluator multiplicity is unresolved, and production RT remains gated.
**Edition 3.339 G3 complex-CSI interval multiplicity correction (2026-10-24):** The numerical scorer now applies a Bonferroni 95% family across the cluster-bootstrap p95 bound and exact-binomial cluster-failure bound for every preregistered scene-family × LOS/NLOS stratum. With one stratum, each one-sided bound uses 97.5% confidence and zero observed failures require 72 clusters for the corrected 5% upper limit. The frozen physical e_H limits are unchanged; the scorer remains numerical-only. This interval family is local to complex CSI and is not integrated with the separate G3.03 material claims or their acquisition design; complete cross-evaluator multiplicity registration and matching sample-size assurance remain open. Seventeen focused scorer tests pass; no measurement data were used and no gate closes.
**Edition 3.338 G5.09 acquisition-guard truth-leakage correction (2026-10-24):** The first independent-channel ordered-impairment report supplied each realized channel's actual echo delay to synchronization, leaking channel truth; it is retained only as a diagnostic. Fixed-guard development diagnostics on the same 64 channels show the split fixed protocol (2-sample timing guard, 6-sample family-maximum CFO guard) avoids per-channel truth but fails frozen limits: timing error −2 in 5/512 blocks; CFO P95/max 263.71/441.05 Hz against required zero timing error and <250/350 Hz. Clean/corrected/uncorrected BLER is 5/512, 48/512, and 512/512; the corrected-minus-clean paired channel-bootstrap 95% interval is [0.0605, 0.1094]. The alternative fixed-maximum and fixed-2 guards also fail and remain diagnostics. These reused seeds are development-only; do not tune or claim confirmation from them. Any changed protocol requires a fresh independent seed set. G5.09 remains open, thresholds are unchanged, and RT production remains gated. See [MVP status](MVP_STATUS.md) and the hash-bound [split-guard result](results/g5-ordered-impairments-independent-channels-cpu-64x8-20261024.json).
**Edition 3.337 bounded G7.12 lossless-storage check (2026-10-24):** Added a dedicated compressed UMCD episode-store regression that compares source and reopened path coefficients, physical CFR, and target values byte-for-byte across complex64/complex128 and float32/float64, and checks target masks, reasons, and definitions. The exercised NPZ ZIP compression is lossless and applies no quantizer. This verifies the selected in-memory episode-store path only; alternate formats and a complete production corpus still require a storage audit. G7.12 remains open at corpus level.
**Edition 3.336 external n78/n79 channel-data disposition (2026-10-24):** Added two catalog-only candidates for later empirical channel validation: a 3.3–3.8-GHz operational n78 outdoor-to-indoor PDP dataset and a 4.85-GHz double-directional 8×8 Yokohama dataset. Neither is material-specific bistatic scattering evidence and neither closes G3.03. Both Zenodo records have unresolved reuse metadata; no files were downloaded or admitted. See the [G3 source audit](G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md).
**Edition 3.335 bounded G5.09 independent-channel ordered-impairment probe (2026-10-24):** Added a 64-channel × 8-codeword paired CPU PUSCH run for the combined IQ imbalance, phase noise, CFO, timing and AWGN chain. It expands the earlier one-channel fixture and retains channel as the inference unit. The report records clean 5/512, corrected 23/512 and uncorrected 512/512 CRC failures; the paired corrected-minus-clean channel-bootstrap interval is [0.0176, 0.0566]. Timing/reference checks pass, while maximum CFO error is 386.87 Hz and fails the frozen <350-Hz limit (P95 208.70 Hz). This is a conditional synthetic failure, not a deployment estimate or G5.09 pass. Source was uncommitted when run and the report must be regenerated after the implementation commit. See [the result](results/g5-ordered-impairments-independent-channels-cpu-64x8-20261024.json).
**Edition 3.334 G3.03 source-access disposition (2026-10-24):** The source audit now records a public 3.5-GHz thesis with penetration-loss measurements and a 26–36-GHz specular-reflection campaign whose data are commercial. Neither provides the open target-frequency bistatic reflected/diffuse angular data, unit-level uncertainty, and held-out groups required by the frozen material criteria. No transfer or calibration is justified; G3.03 stays open and RT production remains gated.
**Edition 3.333 G3 qualification resource baseline (2026-10-24):** Added a reproducible arithmetic baseline from hash-pinned depth-3 LLVM runs in City41/42/43. For the 354-cluster zero-failure floor, historical median solver time is about 107 seconds for one 100k/1M candidate/challenger link pair per cluster, or 11 minutes for six links; a conservative historical-max envelope is about 13.5/81 minutes. This excludes the needed independent scene families, nonzero calibrated scattering, receiver work, and full raw path/CFR storage. It is not a run-specific estimate or authorization; a representative bounded target-scene pilot and complete artifact inventory remain necessary before any campaign.
**Edition 3.332 G5.09 family-wise profile-matrix audit (2026-10-24):** Added a hash-bound analysis of the five 128-parent TDL-A–E CP-exceedance curves. The audit verifies the shared source snapshot, curve configuration and point hashes, and parent uniqueness, then computes 100,000 paired-parent bootstrap draws per DMRS-LS contrast with Bonferroni family-wise 95% confidence across 15 profile/SNR claims. Positive contrasts remain resolved for TDL-A/B/C at 60 dB and TDL-B/C at 80 dB; other intervals include zero. This is conditional standardized-channel evidence and does not meet the separate G5 receiver or G3 physical qualification criteria.
**Edition 3.331 G5.09 standardized TDL profile sensitivity (2026-10-24):** Added five current-source 128-parent reports for TDL-A through TDL-E at 60/80/100-dB SNR. All provenance and source/config/point hashes, parent uniqueness, and paired cluster-bootstrap intervals were audited; A/C were regenerated because earlier report hashes no longer matched current source. Results show pronounced NLOS A/B/C sensitivity and sparse LOS D/E failures, but the 15 nominal 95% intervals have no across-comparison multiplicity correction. These exploratory outcomes characterize only the fixed standardized diagnostic; they do not qualify G5, establish physical truth, or authorize production RT data.
**Edition 3.330 G3.03 planning guardrails (2026-10-24):** The reproducible planning CLI now fixes family-wise alpha at 0.05, preserving the frozen 95% criterion, and rejects bootstrap candidate sizes below the evaluator 20-unit floor. Both the JSON schema and runtime reject weakened alpha or under-floor candidates. All 39 focused G3 planner/CLI tests pass; mypy, Ruff, formatting, and whitespace checks pass. Reports remain assumption-only and NOT_QUALIFIED.
**Edition 3.329 G3.03 reproducible planning entry point (2026-10-24):** Added a strict JSON-input CLI for the assumption-driven independent-unit and optional evaluator-aligned bootstrap-assurance planners. It derives the stratum count from an explicit, unique material × polarization × incidence list, binds exact input and source hashes, rejects duplicate JSON keys, and always reports NOT_QUALIFIED; the two planning methods remain separate and the tool never invents a combined sample size. The synthetic example yields 12,131 units/stratum under its conservative analytic assumptions while its deliberately simple two-atom bootstrap scenario accepts the 20-unit candidate. This large difference exposes assumption sensitivity; neither value is a measurement-based sample recommendation. Thirty-seven planning and CLI tests pass; production RT and G3 qualification remain gated.
**Edition 3.328 G4.08 named-case Doppler physics check (2026-10-24):** Extended the independent paired-draw temporal-correlation reference to every one of the 17 TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 Annex B.2.2 named TDL profile/Doppler cases. Each uses 8,192 independent channel draws sampled at one quarter of the registered maximum-Doppler period; observed normalized tap autocorrelation agrees with the independent Jakes reference (including the Rician LOS term for TDL-D30) under a 99% Bonferroni family over 34 real/imaginary claims. All 33 original and named-case profile checks pass. Alongside Edition 3.327, this validates native generation, record admission, and temporal coefficient evolution for these named configurations. It remains standardized-model self-consistency evidence, not RF/material validation or deployment receiver evidence.
**Edition 3.327 G4.08 native Annex B.2.2 matrix (2026-10-24):** The native Sionna admission-and-storage test now derives every named case from the pinned registry and exercises all 17 FR1/FR2 profile/Doppler combinations through channel generation, standardized-record writing, reopening, and metadata admission. All 17 CPU cases pass. This closes the adapter-to-record software-conformance coverage gap; it does not validate RF accuracy, arbitrary bandwidth/numerology combinations, physical propagation, or receiver performance.

**Edition 3.326 G5.09 ordered IQ/oscillator receiver fixture (2026-10-24):** Extended the versioned, hash-bound CPU PUSCH impairment chain with TX and RX memoryless IQ imbalance (each 0.5 dB gain imbalance; phase imbalance +0.02/-0.02 rad) in the declared order around the static two-tap channel, TX/RX Wiener phase noise, 600-Hz CFO, capture timing offset, and AWGN. The pre-existing acceptance limits pass on 128 transport blocks: clean 0/128 CRC failures, timing error 0, CFO absolute-error P95/max 185.89/226.80 Hz, corrected 2/128 (1.56%) CRC failures, uncorrected 128/128 failures. A post-hoc one-sided exact 95% upper bound for the corrected block failure rate is 4.84%; it is conditional on this fixed channel and was not a frozen pass criterion. The provenance auditor reports zero issues, but `source_matches_git_revision=false` because implementation changes are uncommitted; regenerate after the implementation commit. This is one synthetic fixed-channel receiver fixture, with no phase-noise tracking or IQ compensation, and does not qualify deployment performance or close G5.09. Artifact: [hash-bound 128-block result](results/g5-ordered-impairments-tx-rx-iq-cpu-128-20261024.json).

**Edition 3.325 G7.08 signed split-lock verification (2026-10-24):** Added `verify_signed_split_policy_registry`, which requires an OpenSSH signature in the dedicated `umcd-split-policy-v2@github.com` namespace and an externally supplied trusted `allowed_signers` file. Lock, signature and trust bytes are snapshotted before verification; invalid bytes, untrusted principals, and hard-linked artifacts fail closed. The signing workflow and institutional key-custody boundary are documented. Fifty-three split-policy tests pass, including signature tampering cases; this enables prospective signed locks but does not sign or qualify a corpus. G7.08 remains open until a final candidate corpus is frozen, audited, independently trusted and signed before tuning.

**Edition 3.324 G5.09 finite-symbol Doppler reference and coefficient precision (2026-10-24):** Fixed `apply_time_varying_multipath` so Python complex path coefficients are cast directly to the waveform dtype after complex-input validation; this avoids silently narrowing double-precision coefficients to complex64 before widening. Added a full 64-bin FFT comparison against an independently summed finite-symbol reference at 0, 0.025, 0.1, and 0.2 Doppler/SCS for a delayed path with a 16-sample CP. The existing impairment and waveform suites pass (31 tests); Ruff check and formatting pass. This validates the sample-domain implementation in the named analytic fixture only. It does not qualify a mobile NR receiver or isolate deployment ICI impact; G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.323 G4.08 named fixed-profile Doppler-case admission (2026-10-24):** Added a version-pinned registry for all 17 TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 Annex B.2.2 combinations: profile model, FR1/FR2, and maximum Doppler. The TDL adapter can take a named case, derives `max_speed = f_D,max c / f_c` when omitted, and binds the case ID and realized speed/Doppler into channel provenance. Standardized-record admission independently rechecks the case/profile, frequency range, derived speed, and recorded Doppler, rejecting mismatches or tampered metadata. Pure admission tests cover all 17 cases and five mismatch modes; native Sionna tests cover FR1 `TDLA30-5` and FR2 `TDLC60-300`, including case metadata through record construction. This closes the named Annex B.2.2 profile/Doppler combinations only; arbitrary speed ranges remain generic simulations and are not labeled as those conformance cases. G4.08 remains partial for broader test-point, bandwidth/numerology and physical-channel coverage; no field or G3 qualification is implied.

**Project:** 5G NR Channel Condition / Multipath State Intelligence
**Planning date:** 2026-10-24 (Europe/Helsinki)
**Current plan edition:** 3.377 - 2026-10-24 (joint G3 claim allocation is now reflected in assumption-only planning; assumptions and external evidence remain unverified, as do institutional split-lock trust, corpus-wide lineage, G7.12 storage audit and production RT)
**Status:** design specification; **not** a generated dataset, empirical result, simulator-accuracy certification, or promise of a top-tier publication.
**Plan-finalization boundary (edition 3.263):** This is the complete staged execution specification for the currently chosen project scope. Remaining gates and evidence-dependent choices are explicitly open; this document does not claim that research execution, qualification, or dataset release is complete.
**Production D1 gate:** Production generation is enforced as fail-closed: the repository has no trusted approval-signature keyring, so production authorization always denies. Explicit pilot requests are request-bound, numerically bounded, and scratch-output confined to `/tmp`. Pilot execution is not production qualification, and no production D1 corpus may be generated until the scientific acceptance criteria and trusted approval mechanism are satisfied.
**Edition numbering:** This document and the Master Research Plan maintain independent edition sequences.

**Edition 3.317 G5.09 curve configuration provenance v4 and G0.07 rescan (2026-10-24):** The paired probe/CLI now accept TDL-A–E, keep A as the default, and write non-A outputs to profile-specific paths. Curve schema v4 includes the full frozen channel/receiver configuration inside the configuration-hashed record, including profile, delay spread, maximum excess delay, CP, SCS, bandwidth, resource-block allocation, and SNR definition. Regenerated 128-parent v4 TDL-A and TDL-C curves exactly reproduce their prior outcomes and parent lineages; each report passes the repository provenance auditor, and source/configuration/point digests plus parent hashes verify. TDL-C paired DMRS-LS time-domain-minus-ideal-CFR BLER differences are 0.352 [0.273, 0.438], 0.266 [0.172, 0.367], and 0.117 [0.000, 0.234] at 60/80/100 dB; only the first two intervals exclude zero. The full results-tree scan finds 190 valid JSON files, zero malformed files, and 185 legacy files with provenance gaps (missing command 185, UTC timestamp 180, config hash 180, source hash 63); G0.07 remains open and historical fields were not fabricated. Focused experiment tests pass (8), Ruff and mypy pass; the repository-wide suite was not rerun. Artifacts: [TDL-A v4 curve](results/g5-standardized-pusch-cp-exceedance-tdl-a-snr-curve-128-v1-20261024.json) and [TDL-C v4 curve](results/g5-standardized-pusch-cp-exceedance-tdl-c-snr-curve-128-v2-20261024.json).

**Edition 3.318 criterion-oriented G3 complex-CSI scorer (2026-10-24):** Added `evaluation/g3_complex_csi.py` and focused contract tests. It implements the frozen symmetric unnormalized complex-CFR error without phase alignment, computes equal-cluster-weighted median/p95 diagnostics, a cluster-bootstrap one-sided p95 upper bound, and the exact one-sided binomial upper bound for clusters with any episode above `e_H=0.05`. A preregistered expected family × LOS/NLOS set is required and must exactly match observed strata; stable grid/configuration IDs and identical CFR shape and `H_floor` are required across the comparison. Confidence bounds are marginal per stratum, without cross-stratum multiplicity correction. Invalid/mismatched arrays, duplicate episodes, and insufficient clusters fail closed. Bootstrap index allocation is chunked to bound temporary memory. Even when numeric checks pass, the report remains `NUMERICAL_CRITERIA_ONLY_NOT_QUALIFIED`; actual axis/phase/noise-floor correctness, physical independence, provenance, and representative scene-family evidence require independent validation. All 16 focused tests pass; the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 103 tests; Ruff, format, and targeted mypy pass. This is evaluator scaffolding only and authorizes no G3 qualification or RT production run.

**Edition 3.319 criterion-oriented G3 delay/angular scorer (2026-10-24):** Added `evaluation/g3_propagation_metrics.py` for paired first-arrival delay, RMS delay spread, and 3D arrival/departure angular-spread diagnostics. It normalizes errors by the frozen tolerances (0.1/B_eff, max(1 ns, 5%), and max(1 degree, 5%)), reports equal-cluster-weighted median/p95 values, a chunked cluster-bootstrap one-sided p95 upper bound, and exact cluster-failure bounds. It requires an exact preregistered family × LOS/NLOS × total/specular/diffuse stratum set; unresolved values require reasons, and not-applicable components remain masked rather than scored as zero. Confidence bounds are marginal per metric/stratum, without multiplicity correction. All 9 focused tests pass; the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 112 tests, and Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. Outputs remain numerical-only and cannot qualify propagation or authorize production RT.

**Edition 3.320 reusable G3 path-equivalence matching (2026-10-24):** Added `evaluation/g3_path_equivalence.py` to preserve every explicitly solver-valid deterministic/specular path record, form representative-bounded effective classes from ordered interaction/object signatures plus frozen vertex/delay/direction resolutions, and match classes one-to-one. Primitive IDs are retained as descriptive metadata rather than permanent identity; diffuse stochastic paths are rejected from identity matching. Class aggregates retain raw multiplicity and coherent CFR contribution, including repeated zero-interaction records. Receiver relevance follows the frozen criteria: isolated class omission changes active-grid `e_H` by at least 0.01 or post-link-budget class power is at least 6 dB above equivalent receiver noise. The comparison reports both candidate-to-challenger and challenger-to-candidate relevant-class coverage and enumerates unmatched relevant classes. Twelve focused tests pass; the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 124 tests; Ruff, format, and targeted mypy pass. The matcher is a single-comparison diagnostic with caller-supplied resolutions and no independent-scene confidence or physical qualification; no new RT run was performed.

**Edition 3.321 criterion-oriented G3 receiver-impact scorer (2026-10-24):** Added `evaluation/g3_receiver_impact.py` for paired candidate/challenger PUSCH CRC outcomes on a frozen SNR grid. It requires exact family × LOS/NLOS strata, complete configuration/block/SNR pairing, at least 20 independent clusters per stratum (a pilot floor, not qualification evidence), and preserves decoder-outcome counts separately by configuration. Confidence intervals use a hierarchical bootstrap that resamples channel clusters and then paired transport blocks within each selected cluster. The scorer checks the preregistered common-SNR BLER margin and precision, and computes SNR@10% BLER only for unique monotone grid-bracketed crossings, with no extrapolation. A canonical SHA-256 binds every normalized block/SNR/configuration CRC and decoder outcome in the input; eight focused tests pass, and the combined G3 scorer/planner/material/acquisition-contract slice passes 132 tests. Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. The report can only be numerical diagnostic evidence: it cannot verify the comparison point was frozen in advance, establish G5 receiver qualification or physical independence, or authorize production RT.

**Edition 3.322 bounded City43 high-depth S=0 write-hook replay (2026-10-24):** Archived one repeat of the already selected center-LOS, seed-23, depth-10, 1M-sample/source case; no sweep or new qualification set was run. It reproduces 597 valid paths and 29,110 pre-validation candidates without filling the 1M per-source cap. The 75 zero-interaction records first increase from 1 after explicit LOS insertion to 75 after the shoot-and-bounce stage, an output-stage increase of 74; the established path-level decomposition still attributes the finite-budget CFR change principally to added returned contributions that add coherently, with matched-path coefficient/geometry drift negligible. The instrumented CFR differs from the clean 1M baseline by relative L2 `2.6734359855e-7` (maximum absolute sample difference `8.3819063295e-9`), supporting non-perturbation for this single replay. The write-hook itself is incomplete: only 1/75 zero-order records carries writer metadata in intermediate candidate snapshots and 60/75 in final `Paths`; exact per-record origin/depth for the remaining records, and the internal reason they arise, therefore remain unresolved. Pinned Sionna 2.2.0 source confirms that both the direct-LOS and shoot-and-bounce paths call `PathsBuffer.add_paths`, but these project-side hooks do not establish which internal branch produced every record. Preserve the result and its provenance at [`rt-city43-center-los-seed23-depth10-s0-lineage-writehook-v2-20261024.json.gz`](results/rt-city43-center-los-seed23-depth10-s0-lineage-writehook-v2-20261024.json.gz). This closes the bounded output-stage investigation only, not G3; City43 is one synthetic scene family, neither finite sample budget is truth, no further budget/depth sweeps are warranted for this question, and production D1/advanced training remain gated by the full acceptance contract.

**Edition 3.316 bounded G5.09 TDL-C CP-exceedance receiver curve (2026-10-24):** Generalized the paired receiver probe from TDL-A to TDL-A–E while preserving the A default and existing report path, then ran 128 independent TDL-C channel parents across matched 60/80/100-dB receive-grid SNR points. DMRS-LS time-domain-minus-ideal-CFR BLER differences are 0.352 (95% parent-cluster interval [0.273, 0.438]), 0.266 ([0.172, 0.367]), and 0.117 ([0.000, 0.234]); the first two intervals exclude zero. Every SNR reuses the same 128 channel parents and paired receiver seeds. The report passes the repository provenance audit; all six source hashes, configuration/point digests, and 128 unique parent/truth hashes were independently verified. This is a conditional synthetic TDL-C stress case, not a deployment curve, G5.09 pass, or G3 receiver qualification. The full repository suite was not rerun. Artifact: [g5-standardized-pusch-cp-exceedance-tdl-c-snr-curve-128-v1-20261024.json](results/g5-standardized-pusch-cp-exceedance-tdl-c-snr-curve-128-v1-20261024.json).

**Edition 3.315 verified G4.07 joint TX/RX covariance matrix and reconciled gate record (2026-10-24):** Re-ran the native CPU joint covariance matrix for CDL-A–E across 2×2, 4×4, 1×4, and 4×1 TX/RX arrays; all 20 cases pass. A single 99% simultaneous family covers 1,520 unique real Hermitian components, with 16,384 independent draws per profile/topology and maximum normalized-power interval half-width 0.05. The G4.07 gate text now matches this implementation. It remains pinned standardized-model consistency, not arbitrary-array or physical-channel validation.

**Edition 3.314 bounded G4.07 aligned CDL-D/E port-power precision (2026-10-24):** Added aligned-orientation dual-VH omni port-power checks after the original 4,096-draw matrix failed the fixed 25%-of-minimum-expected-power precision cap. A separate 4,096-draw pilot (seeds 525/526) estimated required counts of 208,786/8,562; the fixed confirmation uses 262,144 CDL-D and 16,384 CDL-E independent draws in 32,768-sample chunks with distinct deterministic seeds. All eight aligned port/profile expectations lie within 99% family-wise intervals under one 40-claim family spanning aligned and four rotated orientation pairs; maximum half-widths are 0.001974 (D, cap 0.002227) and 0.003052 (E, cap 0.004191). The aligned tests pass, as do all eight rotated cases after applying the same family size. This is standardized-model consistency for the named dual-VH omni setup, not arbitrary antenna-pattern, geometric-RT, or measured-channel qualification.

**Edition 3.313 bounded G5.01 Type-2 single-symbol scheduled waveform and CRC recovery (2026-10-24):** Added a 40-case native CPU transmitter/receiver matrix for partial allocations of 10–14 symbols, mappings A/B, and dmrs-AdditionalPosition 0–3. Each allocation is scheduled in a 14-symbol slot, OFDM-modulated and demodulated, extracted, and decoded with exact payload and transport-block CRC recovery. All cases pass. This is noiseless implementation evidence, not noisy receiver qualification or full standards conformance; G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.312 bounded G5.01 Type-2 single-symbol partial DM-RS timing (2026-10-24):** The native resource-mask test covers allocation durations 10–14, mapping A/B, and all four dmrs-AdditionalPosition values against the independently transcribed TS 38.211 V19.1.0 Table 6.4.1.1.3-3, including the Type-2 frequency comb. All 40 focused cases pass. This closes one allocation-length coverage gap only; full-slot standards coverage, hopping, broader port/CDM combinations, noisy receiver effects, and complete conformance remain open.

**Edition 3.311 G7.11 ODbL release artifact snapshot integrity (2026-10-24):** The public exporter now opens each artifact through source-root-relative directory descriptors without following symlinks, requires a single-link regular file, and streams bytes from that same descriptor into the temporary ZIP while computing SHA-256. A mismatch aborts before atomic publication, so the archive cannot contain a second pathname read that escaped the manifest hash check; noncanonical paths, generated-metadata name collisions, and symlink/hard-link artifacts are rejected. The 20 public-release tests pass, including same-inode mutation after descriptor open while preserving an existing destination archive; Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. This closes an exporter integrity race, not the ODbL rights review: source extract/conversion provenance and third-party closure remain incomplete, so no present scene-derived artifact is cleared for public release.

**Edition 3.310 bounded G4.07 rotated CDL-D/E polarization orientation matrix (2026-10-24):** Expanded the dual-VH omni LOS port-power reference from one to four frozen TX/RX orientation pairs: TX-only tilt, RX-only tilt, both-end tilt, and a distinct crossed pair. An independent TR 38.901 field-basis/XPR/K-factor reference is compared with 4,096 CPU channel draws/profile for all four port pairs. The 32 claims use one 99% Bonferroni family; every reference lies within its interval and the maximum half-width stays within 25% of the smallest expected port-pair power. All eight new rotated profile/orientation cases pass; the full dual-polarization test file passes 13 tests, with Ruff and formatting clean. The aligned near-zero LOS cross-polar component did not meet this relative-precision cap at 4,096 draws; edition 3.314 records separate fixed-budget per-port confirmation under the unchanged cap. No threshold was relaxed. This remains bounded standardized-model consistency, not arbitrary antenna-pattern, geometric-RT, or physical-channel validation. The repository-wide suite has not been rerun.

**Edition 3.309 bounded G5.01 Type-2 partial-allocation scheduling and decoding (2026-10-24):** Extended the Type-2 double-symbol partial-allocation matrix into a native transmitter/receiver path for all 20 combinations of lengths 10–14, mapping A/B, and `dmrs-AdditionalPosition` 0/1. Each generated allocation is inserted at its configured position in a 14-symbol slot, OFDM-modulated, demodulated, extracted, and decoded with exact payload and transport-block CRC recovery. All 20 cases pass; the complete PUSCH integration file passes 215 tests, with Ruff and formatting clean. This is noiseless scheduling/implementation evidence for the named Type-2 configurations; it does not qualify noisy channel estimation, MIMO, frequency hopping, transform precoding, or receiver performance and does not close G5.01. The repository-wide suite has not been rerun.

**Edition 3.308 bounded G5.01 Type-2 double-symbol DM-RS partial allocations (2026-10-24):** Expanded `tests/test_integrations/test_nr_pusch_native.py::test_pusch_type2_double_symbol_partial_allocation_matches_ts_38211` across allocation durations 10–14, mapping A/B, `dmrs-AdditionalPosition` 0/1, and 15/30/60/120-kHz subcarrier spacing. Expected symbol groups follow TS 38.211 V19.1.0 Table 6.4.1.1.3-4; the test checks allocation-relative masks, configured allocation metadata, and the Type-2 frequency comb. All 80 parameter combinations pass, and the complete PUSCH integration file passes 196 tests; changed-file Ruff and formatting pass. A direct mypy invocation on this test module reports 15 existing typing errors elsewhere in the file, none on this test. This is native resource-grid evidence, not full transmitter/receiver qualification; other allocation lengths, CDM/port combinations, hopping, transform precoding, and end-to-end receiver impact remain open, and G5.01 is not closed. The repository-wide suite has not been rerun.

**Edition 3.307 bounded G4.07 delay/angular moments across CDL-A–E (2026-10-24):** Extended the hash-pinned delay/cos(AoA) statistic to AoD, ZoA, and ZoD for the five CDL profiles. The independent references use each profile's cluster angles, angular spreads, PDP, and 20 ray offsets; CDL-D/E retain the power-weighted reference for their composed zero-delay LOS/diffuse tap. One Bonferroni family covers all 20 profile/angle claims at 99% simultaneous confidence. Each estimate is within 0.05 of its reference and has interval half-width at most 0.05. The two focused files pass 20 cases; changed-path Ruff, formatting, and mypy pass. These are selected standardized-profile moments, not full angular-distribution, geometric-RT, complex-CSI, or measured-channel qualification. The full repository suite has not been rerun.

**Edition 3.306 bounded G5.09 CP-exceedance receiver curve (2026-10-24):** Extended the existing matched TDL-A time-domain versus ideal-CFR PUSCH diagnostic to 128 independent channel parents reused across 60/80/100 dB post-channel receive-grid SNR points. For DMRS-LS decoding, the paired BLER difference is 0.164 at 60 dB (95% parent-cluster interval [0.102, 0.234]), 0.078 at 80 dB ([-0.016, 0.172]), and 0.031 at 100 dB ([-0.078, 0.133]); only the 60-dB interval excludes zero. The diagonal-CFR oracle diagnostic shows positive time-domain penalties at all three points, but is not a physical receiver model. The deterministic rerun reproduced all point outcomes and parent lineage; the result's provenance audit and source-file hashes pass. This is conditional on the frozen synthetic TDL-A, 3.5-GHz, 30-kHz, 6-RB, 1-µs-delay-spread experiment, not a G5 pass or deployment claim. The full repository suite has not been rerun.

**Edition 3.305 bounded G4.07 rotated LOS polarization matrix (2026-10-24):** Extended the hash-pinned CDL-D/E checks from aggregate power to all four dual-VH port-pair powers at one declared pair of tilted array orientations. The independent reference computes LOS field-basis rotation from the profile LOS angles, and computes diffuse port powers by averaging the TR 38.901 20-ray offsets over independent azimuth/zenith angle couplings before applying the table XPR and K-factor. Across 4,096 channel draws per profile, all eight simultaneous 99% intervals meet their reference; each half-width is at most 25% of the smallest expected port-pair power. The complete focused file passes 7 cases; Ruff, formatting, and targeted mypy pass. This is one dual-VH omni orientation pair and standardized-profile implementation-consistency evidence, not broad antenna, orientation, angular-distribution, geometric-RT, or measured validation. The full repository suite has not been rerun after this extension.

**Edition 3.304 bounded G4.07 dual-polarization CDL-A–E power checks (2026-10-24):** The hash-pinned TR 38.901 v19.2 dual-VH omni XPR test now covers non-LOS CDL-A/B/C per-port-pair powers plus LOS CDL-D/E aggregate two-port Frobenius power. For D/E, an independent reference combines the profile-table K-factor and XPR with the angle-invariant total power of the LOS polarization transform; one 99% simultaneous interval family spans the two LOS profiles, with half-width capped at 1% of expected aggregate power. All five profile cases pass in the focused file (5 tests); Ruff and formatting pass. LOS per-port mixing, general field patterns/orientations, arbitrary arrays, full angular distributions, geometric RT, and measured physical validity remain open. The full repository suite has not been rerun after this extension.

**Edition 3.303 bounded G4.07 non-LOS dual-polarization XPR matrix (2026-10-24):** Extended the independent TR 38.901 v19.2 XPR power reference from CDL-A to CDL-A/B/C, using hash-pinned profile tables and one dual-VH omnidirectional port at each endpoint. Four RX/TX polarization powers per profile are evaluated across 4,096 independent channel draws; one Bonferroni family covers all 12 profile/port-pair intervals at 99% simultaneous confidence, with a maximum half-width of one quarter of the weakest expected cross-polar power. All three profiles pass. The focused test file passes 3 cases; Ruff and formatting pass. This is single-topology non-LOS model-consistency evidence. LOS CDL-D/E polarization mixing, general field patterns/orientations, arbitrary arrays, full angular distributions, geometric RT, and measured physical validity remain open.

**Edition 3.302 latest full-suite verification (2026-10-24):** The unfiltered repository test command completes with exit code 0: 1,737 passed and 3 skipped in 218.81 seconds. The full coverage run on this same Case A-G receiver matrix reports 84.69%, above the repository's 75% threshold. This includes the timestamp-stabilized Split Policy v2 rewrite regression and the new candidate-timed SSB acquisition cases. No RT production generation or qualification campaign was run or authorized by this validation.

**Edition 3.301 bounded G5.14 Case A-G acquisition matrix (2026-10-24):** Extended the seeded blind-acquisition test across all seven TS 38.213 timing cases using candidate-timed 5 ms half-frame captures: Case A candidate 3; Cases B/C candidate 7; and Cases D/E/F/G candidate 63. The matrix covers 15/30/120/240/480/960-kHz SSB numerologies and FR1, FR2, FR2-1, and FR2-2; each capture includes an 11-sample leading offset, −1.5 kHz CFO, a five-sample two-tap channel, and 24 dB AWGN. Every case recovers PCI, absolute timing, CFO, full candidate and half-frame, valid PBCH CRC, and MIB. The PBCH receiver file passes 20 tests and the half-frame file passes 9; changed-path Ruff and formatting pass. This is a small deterministic operating envelope, not BLER/robustness qualification, independent conformance, mobility/array evidence, or G5.14 closure; production D1 remains gated.

**Edition 3.300 bounded G5.14 multi-numerology PBCH acquisition (2026-10-24):** Extended the candidate-timed half-frame receiver tests to Case B at 30 kHz and Case D at 120 kHz. The seeded cases decode the highest candidate index in each configured burst (7 of 8 and 63 of 64) under a 23-sample timing offset, −1.5 kHz CFO, a five-sample two-tap channel, and 24 dB AWGN; PCI, absolute sample timing, CFO, candidate/half-frame, CRC, and MIB are checked. The PBCH receiver and half-frame files pass 25 tests, with Ruff and formatting passing. Cases C/E/F/G, multiple simultaneously transmitted candidates, operating-characteristic curves, mobility/array behavior, and independent receiver validation remain open. This is bounded implementation evidence, not G5.14 closure or authorization for production D1.

**Edition 3.299 bounded G5.14 acquisition from candidate-timed half-frame (2026-10-24):** The native PBCH integration test now places a sparse Case-A candidate-3 SSB into its correct symbol position in an exact 5 ms half-frame and runs the blind PSS/SSS-to-PBCH receiver over the complete capture. With a 23-sample leading offset, 2.5 kHz CFO, a five-sample two-tap channel, and seeded 24 dB AWGN, the receiver recovers the expected PCI, absolute sample offset, CFO, candidate 3, half-frame 1, valid PBCH CRC, and typed MIB. The PBCH receiver and half-frame focused files pass 23 tests; Ruff and formatting pass. This is one controlled one-port capture, not broad acquisition, BLER, mobility, array, or standards qualification; G5.14 and production D1 remain open.

**Edition 3.298 split-policy race fixture and full-suite verification (2026-10-24):** Made the same-inode rewrite race regression deterministic by explicitly advancing the replacement file's modification timestamp, rather than relying on filesystem clock granularity. The split-policy implementation is unchanged. All 49 Split Policy v2 tests pass; the full repository test suite passes with 84.69% coverage, above the 75% threshold. The targeted Ruff check and formatter pass. This validates the repository snapshot and stabilizes the race fixture; it does not strengthen the lock against changes that preserve all observed metadata or establish trusted registry authorship.

**Edition 3.297 bounded G5.14 SSB half-frame assembly (2026-10-24):** Added `nr_ssb_ofdm_modulate_half_frame` to place sparse, candidate-indexed 4×240 SSB resource grids at their Release 19 Case A-G symbol positions in a complete 5 ms half-frame. It derives every normal-CP interval from the absolute symbol clock, checks candidate, cell, half-frame, and grid metadata, leaves untransmitted occasions silent, and returns immutable waveform metadata. Tests check first/last candidate placement for all seven cases, exact sample count, sample-for-sample equality with independently modulated SSB blocks, and zero gaps. The focused PBCH/SSB slice passes 121 tests; changed-path Ruff, format, and mypy pass. This is a one-port centered-grid assembly component; carrier placement, a complete radio-frame waveform, broad acquisition/channel qualification, and calibrated multi-port RF remain open. Sources: [ETSI TS 38.213 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138213/19.01.00_60/ts_138213v190100p.pdf), clause 4.1; [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 4.2 and 5.3.1.
**Edition 3.294 bounded G5.14 configured SSB power mapping (2026-10-24):** Added `scale_nr_ssb_resource_grid_power` to interpret configured `ss-PBCH-BlockPower` as SSS EPRE in dBm and map the SSB resource grid into complex-baseband `sqrt(W)` values; the 0/3-dB PSS-to-SSS assumed ratio is applied after SSS calibration. Checks cover the configured range, SSS EPRE at -60/-20/0/50 dBm, both PSS ratios, equal configured EPRE on SSS/PBCH/DM-RS, and waveform mean-square power with occupied-bin and exact cyclic-prefix energy accounted for. The focused PBCH/SSB/NR-sync slice passes 63 tests; changed-path Ruff, format, and mypy pass. This establishes a deterministic signal-power convention for the declared one-port baseband model, not RF-chain calibration, multi-antenna power allocation, burst power, or G5.14 qualification. Sources: [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf), `ss-PBCH-BlockPower` field; [ETSI TS 38.214 V19.2.0](https://etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138214/19.02.00_60/ts_138214v190200p.pdf), clause 4.1.
**Edition 3.295 bounded G5.14 Release 19 SSB candidate timing (2026-10-24):** Generalized the prior Case-A helper to explicit TS 38.213 V19.1.0 Cases A-G: FR1 Cases A-C (including paired/unpaired Case-C frequency branches) and FR2 Cases D-E plus FR2-2 Cases F-G at 480/960 kHz. Tests compare the complete timing tuples for all seven cases, including all 64 positions in D-G, check spacing/range pairing, and preserve the old Case-A API. Case selection remains caller-supplied because applicability is band-specific; shared-spectrum discovery-burst index mapping remains unsupported. The focused timing, EPRE, PBCH-OFDM, and PBCH-DM-RS slice passes 107 tests; Ruff, format, and mypy pass. This adds candidate timing only; it does not implement CP/sample-clock selection, infer transmitted beam positions, or qualify full-cell acquisition. Normative source: [ETSI TS 38.213 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138213/19.01.00_60/ts_138213v190100p.pdf), clause 4.1.
**Edition 3.296 bounded G5.14 SSB normal-CP/sample-rate configuration (2026-10-24):** Added `nr_ssb_waveform_config` to map the absolute half-frame symbol index and SSB SCS (15–960 kHz) to the 4-symbol block's normal-CP sample lengths, numerology, FFT-derived sample rate, and immutable config. The length calculation follows TS 38.211 V19.1.0's normal-CP formula for all μ=0…6 and validates integer-representable CP lengths for the selected FFT. Tests cover the long-CP locations at symbol 0 and 7·2^μ, all TS 38.213 Case A-G candidate positions, and modulate/demodulate round trips across the supported SCS values; the focused timing/EPRE/PBCH-OFDM slice passes 112 tests, with changed-path Ruff, format, and mypy passing. SSB candidate positions in Cases A-G land on normal short-CP symbols, while boundary tests verify the long-CP rule independently. The FFT size is an explicit simulation input; this does not select a deployment bandwidth/sample clock, construct a full half-frame burst, or qualify broad acquisition. Source: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 4.2 and 5.3.1.
**Edition 3.293 bounded G4 CDL rectangular TX/RX covariance (2026-10-24):** Extended the hash-pinned independent TR 38.901 v19.2 joint ray-law reference and native CPU Monte Carlo checks to rectangular 1×4 SIMO and 4×1 MISO ULAs, retaining the existing 2×2 and 4×4 cases. One Bonferroni family covers 1,520 unique real Hermitian covariance components across CDL-A–E and all four topologies; 16,384 independent draws per profile/topology satisfy the 99% simultaneous interval inclusion and maximum 0.05 normalized-power half-width criteria in all 20 cases. The checks preserve distinct TX/RX axes and LOS steering factors. This extends standardized-model consistency only; beam/codebook performance, arbitrary arrays, geometric RT, and physical channel accuracy remain unqualified. The focused native test file passes. G3/D1 gates are unchanged.
**Edition 3.292 bounded G5.14 relative SS/PBCH EPRE profile (2026-10-24):** Added `scale_nr_ssb_resource_grid_epre`, which applies the TS 38.213 clause 4.1 assumed PSS-to-SSS EPRE ratio of 0 or 3 dB while leaving SSS, PBCH data, and PBCH DM-RS unchanged at the reference EPRE. Independent tests verify linear-power ratios, unchanged non-PSS REs, the 0-dB identity case, and invalid-input rejection. The PBCH/SSB/NR-sync focused suite passes 166 tests; Ruff/format and mypy pass on the changed paths. This is relative EPRE scaling only: it does not convert configured SS/PBCH block power to absolute waveform amplitude, select numerology/CP, or establish calibrated transmit power. G5.14 and production D1 remain open. Normative relative assumptions: [ETSI TS 38.213 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138213/19.01.00_60/ts_138213v190100p.pdf), clause 4.1.
**Edition 3.291 bounded G5.14 single-SSB OFDM waveform (2026-10-24):** Added reusable NumPy OFDM modulation/demodulation for one centered 4×240 SSB grid with explicit per-symbol cyclic-prefix lengths, and routed the known-occasion PBCH receiver through the shared demodulator. On CPU, three FFT sizes (256/512/1024) and unequal per-symbol CP vectors match the pinned Sionna 2.2.0 `OFDMModulator`; the inverse recovers all active grid values. The complete PBCH/NR-sync focused suite passes 159 tests, with Ruff/format and targeted mypy passing. The API intentionally leaves numerology/sample-rate selection, standardized CP derivation, TS 38.213 power scaling, carrier offset, and burst placement to the caller. This is a single-block waveform implementation check, not broad waveform/receiver qualification; G5.14 and production D1 remain open.
**Edition 3.290 bounded G5.14 blind candidate-index recovery (2026-10-24):** Blind acquisition now searches only the DM-RS-visible candidate-index values (four or eight low-bit hypotheses), then reconstructs and range-checks the full candidate index from the CRC-valid PBCH payload for counts 10/20/64. It also requires the payload half-frame bit to match the searched half-frame, preventing duplicate hypotheses when DM-RS/data sequences do not depend on half-frame. Seeded 24-dB, five-sample-echo captures recover the exact highest candidate index for counts 8, 10, 20, and 64; the existing Lmax=4 timing/CFO case still passes. The combined PBCH/NR-sync slice passes 156 tests, Ruff/format pass, and targeted mypy passes. This validates a bounded acquisition implementation path, not general receiver performance or broad standards conformance; G5.14 and production D1 remain open.
**Edition 3.289 bounded G5.14 PBCH candidate-count completion (2026-10-24):** Extended physical PBCH scrambling, PBCH-DM-RS initialization, known-occasion OFDM reception, and the acquisition API from counts 4/8 to the payload-supported counts 4/8/10/20/64. For counts above four, the data-scrambler offset and DM-RS initialization now use the three least-significant candidate-index bits, including indices above seven. Independent scalar Gold-sequence checks cover the new counts, and noiseless end-to-end receiver tests decode the maximum candidate index for counts 10, 20, and 64. The PBCH/NR-sync focused suite passes 152 tests; Ruff and formatting pass, and mypy passes for the three changed source modules. This closes a candidate-count implementation gap only: blind acquisition qualification remains demonstrated for one Lmax=4 capture, and G5.14, broader receiver qualification, and production D1 remain open. The mapping follows [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 7.3.3.1 and 7.4.1.4.1, with candidate indexing in [ETSI TS 38.213 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138213/19.01.00_60/ts_138213v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.283 joint-family G3 sample-size planning (2026-10-24):** Corrected `plan_g3_independent_acquisition_units` so one Bonferroni family covers all five distributional G3.03 claims plus the complex-CSI cluster-failure claim across every declared stratum. Both family-wise alpha and power are allocated across those six claims per stratum; the result schema advances to v3. The hypothetical minimum-unit lower bound now includes the exact-binomial complex-CSI requirement, and the angular-maximum upper-bound compatibility check compares against that six-claim lower bound. For one stratum, assumed per-unit pass probabilities (0.90, 0.99, 0.90), planned interval coverage 0.95, and planned complex-CSI cluster-failure probability 0.01, the complex-CSI design requires 293 independent outer clusters and the maximum of all six assumption-driven lower bounds is 10,583. These are planning quantities, not a complete sample-size guarantee: the angular pass-probability assumption, evaluator-aligned bootstrap assurance, population sampling frame, and external measurement/provenance evidence remain prerequisites. The complete suite passes 1,616 tests with 3 skipped and 84.69% coverage; CI-scoped Ruff/format and mypy (203 source files) pass. No acquisition data were used; qualification and production D1 remain gated.

**Edition 3.284 bounded G4 CDL-A–E joint TX/RX covariance at 2×2 and 4×4 (2026-10-24):** Extended the independent hash-pinned TR 38.901 v19.2 ray-law reference to jointly qualify all transmit/receive port covariance components for square 2- and 4-element ULAs at both link ends. The family contains 1,360 unique real Hermitian components across five profiles and both array sizes (80 for 2×2 plus 1,280 for 4×4); 16,384 independent native CPU draws per case satisfy the simultaneous 99% confidence inclusion and maximum 0.05 normalized-power half-width criteria in all ten profile/array cases. CDL-D/E preserve the specular LOS steering outer product separately from diffuse terms. This is standardized-model consistency evidence, not geometric RT truth, receiver performance, or broad physical validation. The focused native test file passes; production D1 and broader G4 remain gated.

**Edition 3.285 bounded G5.09 independent-channel ICI decomposition (2026-10-24):** Extended the paired 64-channel, 8-block-per-channel CPU PUSCH probe with a 750-Hz symbol-static Doppler control. Static, symbol-static, and continuous-Doppler cases share each channel draw, payload set, and waveform-noise realization. A three-claim Bonferroni family uses 99% simultaneous coverage; channel draws remain the inference units. Static BLER is 0/512, with a one-sided multiplicity-adjusted channel-prevalence upper bound of 0.0853. Continuous Doppler yields 202/512 failures (0.3945) and passes the frozen static-to-mobile sensitivity criteria. The symbol-static control yields 195/512 (0.3809); the continuous-minus-symbol-static difference is 0.0137 with a 99% channel-cluster bootstrap interval [−0.0078, 0.0430]. This does not meet the frozen minimum 0.10 absolute intra-symbol BLER increase and its interval includes zero. The bounded study therefore does not qualify an isolated ICI receiver impact or close G5.09. Reproducible record: `docs/research/results/g5-independent-channel-pusch-ici-20261024.json`; production D1 remains gated.

**Edition 3.286 bounded G5.09 higher-Doppler stress extension (2026-10-24):** Added a 1,500-Hz echo condition to the same 64 independent two-path channel draws, payloads, and noise realizations, preserving paired static, symbol-static, and continuous-time observations at both 750 and 1,500 Hz. One five-claim Bonferroni family covers the reference upper bound, both static-to-mobile effects, and both isolated intra-symbol effects at 99% family-wise confidence. At 1,500 Hz, continuous Doppler produces 385/512 CRC failures (0.7520), while the symbol-static control produces 386/512 (0.7539); the paired continuous-minus-symbol-static change is −0.0020 with a 99% cluster-bootstrap interval [−0.0098, 0]. At 750 Hz, the corresponding change is 0.0137 with interval [−0.0098, 0.0449]. Both mobility effects pass their fixed 0.10 minimum-increase criteria, but neither isolated ICI effect reaches its 0.10 minimum or has a lower interval bound above zero. This is bounded synthetic receiver evidence only; G5.09 and production D1 remain open. Reproducible record: `docs/research/results/g5-independent-channel-pusch-doppler-stress-20261024.json`.

**Edition 3.287 Core-1 G4 evidence reconciliation (2026-10-24):** Re-ran the authoritative native CPU checks for the named Core-1 TDL/CDL matrix and joint CDL-A–E covariance. All 110 cases pass: 50 profile/topology covariance cells, 50 profile/topology path-axis reconstruction cases, and 10 joint TX/RX covariance cases (2×2 and 4×4 arrays). The covariance matrix uses its single 1,535-component simultaneous family; the joint array suite uses its single 1,360-component simultaneous family. This verifies the mandatory named Core-1 matrix evidence and resolves the stale ledger statement that the matrix remained open. It does not establish arbitrary topologies, every polarization/time configuration, geometric RT truth, or external physical accuracy; those broader qualifications remain open.

**Edition 3.288 split-lock creation durability (2026-10-24):** Immutable split-registry creation now fsyncs the containing directory after publishing the hard-linked lock and again after removing the temporary hard-link name. On POSIX filesystems that support directory fsync, this persists both the canonical lock name and its final single-link state across a crash; file contents were already fsynced before publication. A focused regression verifies file/directory fsync ordering and the final link count. The 49 Split Policy v2 tests pass; Ruff, formatting, and mypy pass for the changed paths. This adds crash-durability evidence, not trusted authorship/signing or scientific split-independence qualification.

**Edition 3.282 independent-channel G5.09 receiver sensitivity (2026-10-24):** A separate preregistered CPU PUSCH study pairs static and 750-Hz echo-Doppler reception across 64 independent seeded two-path channels, with the same eight payloads and waveform-noise realization within each channel. Each channel has equal influence; the paired effect interval resamples whole channel clusters. Baseline is 0/512 CRC failures; the baseline's one-sided 99.5% Clopper-Pearson upper bound uses the fraction of channel draws with any failure (0/64), yielding 0.0795 as a conservative upper bound on mean BLER. Mobile BLER is 202/512 (0.395); paired mean increase is 0.395 with a 99.5% cluster-bootstrap interval [0.242, 0.553]. The frozen criteria pass for this explicit synthetic channel distribution. This evidence reduces dependence on a single fixed channel but does not isolate intra-symbol ICI, establish field/deployment performance, or close G5.09. Reproducible report: `docs/research/results/g5-independent-channel-pusch-20261024.json`; production D1 remains gated.

**Edition 3.281 bounded blind G5.14 PSS/SSS-to-PBCH chain (2026-10-24):** Connected the existing PSS/SSS synchronizer to the known-occasion PBCH OFDM receiver and searched the full Lmax=4 candidate/half-frame set using BCH CRC. In one seeded capture with 23-sample leading timing, 2.5-kHz CFO, a 5-sample two-tap echo and 24-dB transmit-referenced AWGN, the receiver recovered the exact PCI, integer timing, CFO within 100 Hz, correct SSB candidate/half-frame, valid BCH CRC, SFN, and typed MIB. The combined PBCH and NR-sync suites pass 143 tests; full-tree Ruff, formatting (365 files), mypy (202 sources), uv lock --check, and uv build pass. This single controlled integration case is not broad synchronizer or BLER qualification; additional numerologies, burst patterns, channels, SNRs, mobility, multiple antennas, standardized waveform/power, and physical validation remain open. The full repository suite has not been rerun after this addition; production D1 stays gated.
**Edition 3.280 bounded G5.14 known-occasion OFDM receiver (2026-10-24):** Added a one-antenna receiver for an SSB occasion with known cell ID, candidate, half-frame and exact symbol timing. It demodulates four 256-point OFDM symbols, estimates each PBCH-bearing symbol's frequency response from PBCH-DM-RS with linear interpolation, equalizes the 432 data REs, forms variance-scaled LLRs, descrambles, Polar-decodes, recovers timing, and parses the typed MIB. It passes a near-noiseless flat case and 16 seeded 24-dB AWGN trials with two-tap delays 1–5 samples inside the 18-sample CP; all CRCs and MIBs recover. The preceding integration milestone passed 1,611 tests with 3 skips and 85.06% coverage; full-tree Ruff, formatting (364 files), mypy (201 source files), uv lock --check, and uv build passed. The full suite has not been rerun after this acquisition addition. This is a controlled, known-occasion component result, not a BLER curve or qualification of standardized SSB waveform/power, blind acquisition, timing/CFO search, mobility, multiple antennas, or field performance. G5.14 and production D1 remain gated. Normative channel and PBCH procedures: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.01.00_60/ts_138212v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.279 bounded G5.14 BCCH-BCH MIB interpretation (2026-10-24):** Added strict serialization and parsing for the 24-bit BCCH-BCH MIB alternative: the leading CHOICE bit, six SFN MSBs, enumerated fields, bounded integer fields, and spare bit. The native ideal chain now serializes typed MIB fields, traverses PBCH generation/coding/modulation/decoding, recovers the 24 bits, and parses the same fields. This integration exposed and fixed a payload-boundary error: SFN MSBs are bits 1–6 after the MIB CHOICE bit, and payload recovery now reconstructs SFN from those positions. The focused PBCH suite passes 119 tests, including independent field-layout and invalid-input cases. This is a fixed-schema decoder, not general ASN.1/PER or messageClassExtension support; noisy physical reception, channel estimation, power scaling, OFDM and broad timing/beam validation remain open. G5.14 and production D1 remain gated. References: [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.212 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.01.00_60/ts_138212v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.278 bounded G4 CDL-D/E delay-angle moment (2026-10-24):** Added a native CPU check for the PDP-weighted delay/cos(AoA) cluster moment on hash-pinned CDL-D/E v19.2 profiles. Its independent reference treats the zero-delay tap as the power-weighted composition of the specular LOS component and the first diffuse cluster, whose angular feature averages the 20 TR 38.901 ray offsets; this matches the ensemble-power observable after tap composition and does not claim geometric ray truth. With 8,192 independent draws per profile, both D and E pass absolute reference error <=0.05 and confidence half-width <=0.05 under the same Bonferroni-adjusted 99% family-wise rule spanning all CDL-A-E cases. Together with the A/B/C companion, this closes that one five-profile standardized-model statistic only; full angular validity, complex CSI, geometric RT, receiver impact, and broad physical qualification remain open. Production D1 remains gated. Normative ray offsets and CDL model definitions: [ETSI TR 38.901 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_tr/138900_138999/138901/19.02.00_60/tr_138901v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.277 bounded G5.14 PBCH payload recovery (2026-10-24):** Added receiver-side payload recovery that reads the unscrambled SFN timing bits to infer the payload-scrambling offset, then reverses payload scrambling and interleaving and returns serialized MIB bits, full SFN, half-frame, and transmitted candidate-index prefix fields. A native round trip verifies the complete ideal PBCH bit chain from generated payload through BCH coding, TS 38.211 physical scrambling, QPSK hard decisions, BCH CRC decoding, and payload recovery. The BCH adapter now makes an owned writable copy before PyTorch tensor conversion, avoiding unsafe conversion of the immutable payload array. ASN.1 MIB parsing, noisy/channel-affected reception, synchronization, estimation, power scaling, OFDM, beam timing, PRS/SRS, and independent end-to-end conformance remain open; G5.14 and production D1 remain gated.
**Edition 3.276 bounded G5.14 PBCH payload processing (2026-10-24):** Added the TS 38.212 32-bit PBCH payload generator for serialized 24-bit MIB input, SFN/half-frame/candidate-index timing fields, the category-dependent payload interleaver, and payload scrambling/descrambling for Lmax 4/8/10/20/64. Tests compare the full output against an independent scalar interleaver and Gold-sequence reference over every supported candidate count and multiple SFNs. This now feeds the BCH encoder's expected 32-bit input stage; MIB field serialization/ASN.1 recovery, physical descrambling/demodulation/channel estimation, power scaling, OFDM, timing/beam receiver qualification, PRS/SRS, and independent end-to-end conformance remain open. G5.14 and production D1 remain gated. Normative reference: [TS 38.212 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.01.00_60/ts_138212v190100p.pdf), clauses 7.1.1–7.1.2.
**Edition 3.275 Release Split Policy v2 lock-read stability (2026-10-24):** Split-lock reads now compare device/inode, mode, link count, size, modification time, and change time across the initial path stat, opened descriptor, and post-read descriptor/path stats; they also require the bytes read to match the original size. This rejects a same-inode, same-length rewrite after open even when the replacement remains valid JSON and retains the embedded canonical digest. A focused race regression covers the case. This is local integrity hardening, not authorship or a trusted signature; no split assignment semantics changed.
**Edition 3.274 G3.03 scenario-conditional bootstrap assurance planning (2026-10-24):** Added a bounded nested Monte Carlo planner that mirrors the evaluator's equal-unit point summaries and Bonferroni percentile cluster-bootstrap acceptance checks for power-error quantiles, angular maximum, interval coverage, and prediction-interval width. Callers supply hypothetical finite-support IID summary distributions per expected stratum; one-sided exact Clopper–Pearson lower bounds are multiplicity-adjusted across candidate sizes/strata, and a union bound combines strata without assuming cross-stratum independence. A conformance test compares the planner's acceptance decisions directly with the evaluator. This is conditional planning for supplied assumptions only: scenario provenance and population representativeness are unverified, the candidate grid does not prove a global minimum, and the complete G3.03 design/qualification remains open. No measurement data are inferred, and production D1 generation remains gated.
**Edition 3.273 bounded G4 joint TX/RX covariance extension (2026-10-24):** Extended the independent ray-law reference from CDL-A/B/C to hash-pinned CDL-A–E v19.2. CDL-D/E are evaluated as a specular LOS steering outer product plus independently coupled diffuse-cluster covariance terms; one Bonferroni-adjusted 99% family-wise interval rule covers all 80 unique real Hermitian components across five profiles, using 8,192 independent native CPU draws per profile. Every component passes, with interval half-widths ≤0.05 normalized power. This is a stationary, zero-oriented, single-polarization, half-wavelength 2×2 standardized-model consistency result; the remaining Core-1 observables, other array/polarization/time configurations, geometric RT, and measured validity remain open. No production RT or training gate changes.
**Edition 3.272 bounded G5.14 PBCH BCH decoding (2026-10-24):** Added a pinned Sionna 2.2.0 BCH decoder for batches of 864 physically descrambled PBCH LLRs. The adapter combines PBCH's 352 repeated-bit LLRs, delegates downlink Polar rate recovery and CRC-aided list decoding to Sionna, and returns the 32-bit transport block with per-block CRC status. Native noiseless and seeded noisy round trips recover all eight payloads with valid CRC. This does not implement payload assembly/interleaving/scrambling, physical descrambling/demodulation/channel estimation, MIB recovery, power scaling, OFDM, or receiver timing/beam qualification; G5.14 and production D1 remain gated. Normative parameters: [TS 38.212 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.01.00_60/ts_138212v190100p.pdf), clauses 7.1.3–7.1.5.
**Edition 3.271 bounded G5.14 PBCH BCH channel coding (2026-10-24):** Added a native Sionna 2.2.0 adapter for PBCH BCH channel coding from a 32-bit transport-block sequence that has already undergone TS 38.212 payload generation/interleaving and payload scrambling. Sionna supplies CRC24C, downlink Polar input interleaving, the N=512 mother code, and sub-block interleaving; the adapter applies the BCH E=864 repetition rate matcher by repeating the first 352 bits of the 512-bit output. A native test compares the 512-bit codeword against Sionna's same configured 5G encoder and independently checks the 864-bit repetition mapping. This bridges coding into the existing TS 38.211 physical-channel scrambler, QPSK mapper, and 4×240 grid mapper. Payload assembly/interleaving/scrambling, decoder/MIB recovery, TS 38.213 power scaling, OFDM waveform, receiver timing/beam validation, and PRS/SRS remain open; G5.14 and production D1 remain gated. Normative parameters: [TS 38.212 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138212/19.01.00_60/ts_138212v190100p.pdf), clauses 7.1.3–7.1.5.
**Edition 3.270 bounded G4 joint TX/RX spatial covariance extension (2026-10-24):** The independent joint four-port covariance reference now covers hash-pinned CDL-A/B/C NLOS tables on the same zero-oriented, single-polarization, half-wavelength 2×2 downlink. One family-wise 99% Bonferroni interval rule spans all 48 unique real Hermitian covariance components across the three profiles; all pass over 8,192 independent native CPU draws per profile, with interval half-widths ≤0.05 normalized power. This remains a named standardized-model consistency check, not qualification of other topologies, CDL-D/E, polarization, time evolution, geometric RT, or measured channels. No production RT or training gate changes.
**Edition 3.269 bounded G4 joint TX/RX spatial covariance (2026-10-24):** Added an independent ray-law reference for the joint four-port covariance of a CDL-A 2×2 downlink channel using the hash-pinned TR 38.901 V19.2 profile table, 20-ray angular offsets, and independently randomized angle coupling. Across 8,192 independent native CPU draws, all 16 unique real components of the Hermitian covariance matrix pass one 99% Bonferroni family-wise interval rule, with interval half-widths ≤0.05 normalized power. This closes only the named stationary, zero-oriented, single-polarization, half-wavelength 2×2 model-consistency case; other profiles/topologies, polarization, time evolution, geometric RT, and measured validity remain open. No production RT or training gate changes.
**Edition 3.268 bounded G5.14 PBCH data modulation (2026-10-24):** Added TS 38.211 PBCH bit scrambling and QPSK mapping for the 864 rate-matched data bits that feed the existing 4×240 SSB grid mapper. The scrambler uses the cell identity as `c_init` and the candidate-index-dependent `v × M_bit` Gold-sequence offset (with `M_bit=864`); independent scalar Gold-recurrence tests cover four- and eight-candidate configurations, and QPSK tests pin bit-to-I/Q sign order. This completes the data-symbol preparation portion of the mapper interface only. BCH CRC/Polar coding and decoding, TS 38.213 power scaling, OFDM waveform generation, timing/beam qualification, and receiver impact remain open; G5.14 and production D1 remain gated.
**Edition 3.267 bounded G5.14 SS/PBCH resource mapping (2026-10-24):** Added `map_nr_ssb_resource_grid` to assemble a 4×240 SS/PBCH-block grid containing PSS, SSS, PBCH-DM-RS, caller-supplied 432 PBCH modulation symbols, and the table-defined zero REs. The PBCH-DM-RS Gold sequence and 144 ordered REs follow the [TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf) clauses 5.2.1, 7.4.1.4.1, and 7.4.3.1.3; mapping uses Table 7.4.3.1-1. Independent tests compare sequence bits with a scalar Gold recurrence, pin representative initialization values, verify all resource placement/counts/order and zero REs, and reject invalid inputs. This is grid mapping only: input PBCH symbols are assumed already scrambled, encoded, and modulated; PBCH/BCH encoding and decoding, TS 38.213 power scaling, OFDM modulation, and SSB timing/beam receiver claims remain open. G5.14 remains open; no receiver or dataset gate is closed by this component.
**Edition 3.265 bounded G4 angular-moment extension (2026-10-24):** The delay/cos(AoA) PDP-weighted moment test now covers hash-pinned TR 38.901 CDL-A/B/C tables using 4,096 independent CPU channel draws per profile. The expected moment is calculated from each profile's normalized cluster powers, normalized excess delays, and 20 azimuth ray offsets; the pass rule retains absolute reference error ≤0.05 and interval half-width ≤0.05, with Bonferroni-adjusted 99% family-wise confidence across all three profiles. All three pass. CDL-D/E remain excluded because their zero-delay specular and diffuse terms are coherently combined by the channel generator, requiring a separately defined observable/reference. This extends one NLOS angular statistic only; it does not establish full angular-distribution, geometric-RT, complex-CSI, or receiver validity. No acceptance margin changed and production RT remains gated.
**Edition 3.264 G4 Core-1 covariance matrix (2026-10-24):** The 50 TDL/CDL profile-by-topology covariance cases now use one Bonferroni family across all matrix cells and all unique real covariance components, with nominal 99% family-wise confidence and the existing 0.05 normalized-power half-width limit. All 50 cases pass on the pinned native CPU environment with 16,384 independent channel draws each. TDL checks the complete first-tap RX×TX covariance under the configured receive-correlation model. CDL checks the RX covariance marginal at TX port 0; it does not establish joint departure-angle/TX-array covariance or polarization behavior. This closes the matrix-wide multiplicity gap for these named observables, not Core-1 or G4 as a whole; remaining profile/topology observables and external validity stay open. No acceptance threshold changed and production RT remains gated.
**Edition 3.263 bounded implementation tranche (2026-10-24):** G3.03 now has a proposal-only v0.2 package contract and semantic verifier. It binds exact inventory bytes and safely opened paths, enforces per-file rights coverage, validates scorer/split/evaluator byte hashes, requires the standalone split manifest to equal the scorer-embedded manifest, checks held-out assignments and acquisition-unit/source-file crosswalks, and checks angular/CSI array dimensions. The verifier always reports `NOT_QUALIFIED`; it cannot prove signatures, independence, calibration truth, rights sufficiency, or experimental validity.
