**Edition 3.215 Type-2 four-layer receiver separation (G5.02 partial):** Added a seeded four-layer PUSCH grid through a fixed known 4×4 complex channel; the native DM-RS estimator recovers all receive-antenna/stream coefficients across all 14×48 resource elements within `1e-6` at zero noise. This is an exact flat-channel receiver fixture, not frequency-selective MIMO, noisy channel estimation, or a claim about RT propagation; G5.02 remains open for those broader cases.

**Edition 3.216 G3.03 material-source audit:** The source audit establishes that ITU-R P.2040 material electrical-property fits do not supply a calibrated Sionna diffuse-scattering coefficient or angular pattern. Published surface-scattering measurements at 8/12/28 GHz are a potential external-validation lead but do not calibrate this project's 3.5-GHz material configurations. G3.03 remains open pending named target-frequency surface evidence or a separately validated frequency-transfer model; synthetic `S=0.5` interventions remain solver-sensitivity controls only. No RT campaign was run for this audit. Details and sources: [`G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md`](G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md).

**Edition 3.217 near-carrier reflection-data lead (G3.03 remains open):** Added a 1996 4.0-GHz limestone/glass/brick reflection-coefficient study as a possible prior for rough-surface reflection modeling near 3.5 GHz. Its frequency, measured observables, and material set do not directly calibrate Sionna's diffuse `S` plus 3D scattering pattern at 3.5 GHz; a validated transfer model and target-frequency validation are still required. The study is a lead, not a gate pass or production authorization.

**Edition 3.218 Type-2 single-symbol DM-RS in partial allocations (G5.01 partial):** Added `tests/test_integrations/test_nr_pusch_native.py::test_pusch_type2_single_symbol_partial_allocation_matches_ts_38211`. Eight native CPU cases cover mapping A and B with 10-symbol allocations, all `dmrs-AdditionalPosition` values, and the Type-2 four-subcarrier comb. The observed time positions match TS 38.211 V19.1.0 Table 6.4.1.1.3-3; subcarrier offsets match Table 6.4.1.1.3-2. The focused file passes 55 tests. This extends partial-allocation coverage to Type-2 single-symbol DM-RS for one allocation length/start per mapping; Type-2 double-symbol partial timing, other allocation lengths, hopping, transform precoding, and full-slot scheduling remain open. G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.219 end-to-end synchronized PUSCH under in-CP multipath (G5.09 partial):** Added `tests/test_integrations/test_nr_waveform_impairments_native.py::test_pusch_cp_acquisition_and_correction_decode_two_tap_payload_on_cpu`. A seeded 6-RB/30-kHz PUSCH waveform receives a static two-tap channel (2-sample echo), 750-Hz CFO, a 2-sample capture timing offset, and 45-dB sample-domain AWGN. CP acquisition recovers the injected timing and CFO within the declared 25-Hz tolerance; after correction, a matched Sionna PUSCH receiver with the analytical two-tap CFR decodes every payload and passes every transport-block CRC. This connects synchronization to receiver outcomes for one static in-CP condition; it uses perfect analytical CSI and does not qualify DM-RS estimation, phase noise/IQ imbalance, mobility, a general SSB-to-PUSCH handoff, or production receiver performance. G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.220 four-layer Type-2 PUSCH decoding (G5.13 partial):** Extended `tests/test_integrations/test_nr_pusch_native.py::test_type2_four_layer_estimator_and_decoder_recover_known_flat_mimo_channel` so its seeded full-rank 4×4 flat channel now verifies both per-RE Type-2 DM-RS channel recovery and exact payload/CRC recovery through Sionna's matched PUSCH decoder using the analytic channel. This closes one noiseless flat-MIMO receiver reference case; it does not establish noisy BLER, frequency-selective MIMO, precoding/codebook coverage, or multipath receiver qualification.

**Edition 3.221 linear DM-RS interpolation against two-tap channel truth (G5.10 partial):** Added `tests/test_integrations/test_nr_pusch_native.py::test_pusch_linear_dmrs_interpolation_tracks_analytical_two_tap_channel_on_all_res`. Across every symbol and subcarrier in one 4-RB static two-tap response, the native LS estimator with linear interpolation has normalized MSE below 0.02 and outperforms the same fixture with nearest-neighbor interpolation. This qualifies one noiseless low-delay interpolation case only; higher delay spreads, Doppler, SNR, pilot densities, and array/channel configurations remain open.

**Edition 3.222 target-frequency scattering-source audit (G3.03 remains open):** Identified a 2007 anechoic study whose abstract reports 3.5-GHz reflection/scattering measurements for several materials, but the accessible records omit the numerical angular/complex results required to fit Sionna's diffuse coefficient and pattern. ITU-R P.1410-6 provides a 3–60-GHz roughness-based scalar, incoherent Lambertian power model, explicitly without phase/polarization; it cannot be mapped directly to coherent complex RT truth. A 2024 3.5-GHz calibrated measurement-method paper provides a metal-sheet calibration lead, not an accessible construction-material fit. These sources refine the acquisition/measurement path but do not satisfy G3.03; no RT was run and production D1 remains gated. Details are in [`G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md`](G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md).

**Edition 3.223 G0.07 provenance spot-audit:** Verified that the `20261031`/`20261101` suffixes on two inspected analytical result files are numeric seeds: their source revisions and containing commits are dated 2026-10-21, before this audit date. The files explicitly label their evidence as synthetic/descriptive, so they are not classified as future-generated from filename alone. However, they lack stored generation timestamps and exact invocation commands, and one summary's raw-report hash has no tracked raw counterpart. Keep these as diagnostics and do not cite them as fully reproducible benchmark evidence; the remaining result tree is unaudited and G0.07 stays open.

**Edition 3.224 partial-allocation DM-RS absolute symbol index (G5.01 partial):** Added `tests/test_integrations/test_nr_pusch_native.py::test_partial_mapping_b_dmrs_sequence_uses_absolute_slot_symbol_index`. For mapping B with `(start_symbol=3, duration=10)`, native Sionna's pilot values match an independent TS 38.211 Gold-sequence calculation using absolute symbol `l=3`, not allocation-relative `l=0`. The adapter's resource grid remains allocation-local with 10 symbols; full-slot waveform assembly, symbol placement, and slot-context decoding are still open. The focused PUSCH and split-policy test files pass (89 tests); G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.225 frequency-grid slot placement for partial PUSCH (G5.01 partial):** Added `SionnaPUSCHLSReceiver.place_allocation_in_slot` and `extract_allocation_from_slot`. The native CPU test embeds a generated 10-symbol mapping-B PUSCH grid at slot symbols `[3,13)`, checks the unused symbols are zero, extracts the allocation, and recovers all payload bits with valid CRC through Sionna's matched decoder. This is frequency-domain scheduling validation only; time-domain OFDM modulation/slot assembly and multiplexing remain unimplemented. The PUSCH and split-policy files pass (90 tests); G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.226 partial double-symbol DM-RS timing (G5.01 partial):** Added `test_pusch_type2_double_symbol_partial_allocation_matches_ts_38211` with four native CPU cases. For Type-2 double-symbol DM-RS, mapping A `(0,10)` yields symbols `(2,3)` or `(2,3,8,9)` for additional-position 0/1; mapping B `(3,10)` yields allocation-relative symbols `(0,1)` or `(0,1,7,8)`. The four patterns and frequency comb match TS 38.211 V19.1.0 Table 6.4.1.1.3-4. Focused cases pass; G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.227 full-slot OFDM round-trip for scheduled PUSCH (G5.01 partial):** Added `SionnaPUSCHLSReceiver.modulate_slot_grid`. The partial-allocation test now embeds mapping-B PUSCH at slot symbols `[3,13)`, modulates a 14-symbol waveform using the configured Sionna cyclic prefix, demodulates it, checks frequency-grid reconstruction, and verifies exact payload and CRC after allocation extraction. This validates one no-channel frequency/time-domain scheduling path; exact TS cyclic-prefix conformance, channel effects, and multiplexed slots remain outside this check. G5.01 remains open.

**Edition 3.228 scheduled partial-slot PUSCH through an in-CP echo (G5.09 partial):** Added `test_partial_mapping_b_slot_waveform_decodes_static_in_cp_echo`. A seeded mapping-B PUSCH allocation at symbols `[3,13)` is placed in a full slot, OFDM-modulated, passed through a static two-tap sample-domain channel with a two-sample echo inside the configured CP, and corrupted with seeded proper-complex AWGN at variance `1e-5`. After demodulation and allocation extraction, the native DM-RS receiver recovers the payload and CRC. One in-CP static case only; impairment combinations, mobility, repeated BLER curves, and broader link settings remain open.

**Edition 3.229 carrier-relative BWP placement and decode (G5.02 partial):** Added carrier-grid size/start and BWP start fields to `NRPUSCHConfig`, with bounds checks and a backward-compatible default that makes the BWP the full carrier grid. The native CPU test configures a 6-RB BWP at CRB 7 in a 24-RB grid starting at CRB 2, verifies exact placement at bins 60–131, performs a 288-bin full-carrier OFDM round-trip, extracts the BWP-local grid, and recovers payload/CRC. Other offsets, guard/DC conventions, and non-integer-CRB allocations remain open; G5.02's general claim remains bounded to tested cases.

**Edition 3.230 BWP placement matrix across numerologies (G5.02 partial):** Extended `test_bwp_allocation_mapping_roundtrips_at_carrier_grid_edges` to test 4/6/12-RB BWP widths at selected lower, interior, and upper positions in a 24-RB carrier at 15/30/60/120-kHz SCS. Every case checks exact carrier-relative active bins, zero-filled outside regions, extraction back to the allocation, and a full-carrier OFDM modulation/demodulation round-trip with CP scaled from the BWP grid. Focused native PUSCH, receiver-contract, and split-policy tests pass; Ruff, format, mypy, and whitespace checks pass. At this stage, payload/CRC recovery was still shown only for one offset; guard/DC conventions, untested width/offset combinations, fractional allocations, and matrix-wide decoding remained open.

**Edition 3.231 end-to-end BWP placement matrix decode (G5.02 partial):** The 16 selected width/offset/numerology combinations now start from seeded native PUSCH payloads, place the generated grid into the 24-RB carrier, modulate and demodulate the full carrier with CP scaled from the BWP grid, extract the BWP, and pass exact payload and CRC checks through Sionna's matched decoder. This closes matrix-wide decoding for these selected cases, not the full width/offset cross-product. Standardized guard/DC conventions and fractional allocations remain open; G5.02 remains partial.

**Edition 3.232 LS pilot bias/variance across DM-RS and SNR (G5.10 partial):** Expanded the flat-channel Monte Carlo reference to all pilot REs and 18 combinations of Type-1/Type-2 DM-RS, single-/double-symbol DM-RS, one/two reserved CDM groups, and proper-complex noise variance 0.01/0.05/0.2, with 1,024 independent batch draws per case. Empirical LS bias is bounded at each pilot by five predicted standard errors; pooled variance is checked against the closed form `N0/(2 × DMRS_length × reserved_CDM_groups)` and must be within 12%. This validates pilot-site behavior for these declared combinations, not channel interpolation over non-flat/dynamic channels or broad receiver performance; G5.10 remains bounded to tested cases.

**Edition 3.233 linear-interpolator variance calibration diagnostic (G5.10 partial):** Added an 18-case native CPU Monte Carlo check over all REs for a static analytical two-tap channel, Type-1/Type-2 DM-RS, single/double-symbol and additional-position patterns, and noise variance 0.01/0.05/0.2. The empirical estimate mean remains within six standard errors of the noiseless interpolated result. Sionna applies its linear interpolator to the marginal variance tensor rather than propagating full covariance with squared weights; the empirical/reported pooled variance ratio reaches 0.68 in the tested Type-2 case, while the regression bounds the ratio to [0.60, 1.05] across this matrix. `NRChannelEstimate` metadata now labels linear-interpolated variance as approximate, not exact covariance propagation. This identifies a limitation rather than calibrating or correcting the variance; G5.10 remains open for calibrated non-flat interpolation uncertainty and broader delay/Doppler cases.

**Edition 3.234 nearest-neighbor variance reference (G5.10 partial):** The same 18 channel/DM-RS/noise configurations now run with nearest-neighbor interpolation. Across all resource elements with nonzero reported variance, empirical variance matches the reported marginal variance within 12%; the 18 linear cases retain their separate 0.60–1.05 empirical/reported ratio band. Receiver metadata distinguishes nearest-neighbor pilot variance from linearly interpolated marginal variance. Nearest-neighbor's variance behavior is supported for this static analytical matrix, while its channel bias/resolution tradeoff and broader time-varying channels remain open; linear uncertainty remains approximate.

**Edition 3.235 paired interpolation bias/variance tradeoff (G5.10 partial):** For each of six static two-tap channel/DM-RS configurations, the test now compares the noiseless linear and nearest-neighbor channel-estimate NMSE against the exact channel while reusing identical noisy observations across three AWGN variances. Linear interpolation has lower noiseless NMSE in all six tested configurations; nearest-neighbor retains empirical/reported variance agreement within 12%, while linear remains conservative/approximate with its empirical/reported ratio bounded to 0.60–1.05. This supports a measured mode tradeoff in the tested static cases only and does not establish universal ordering or calibrated dynamic-channel uncertainty.
**Edition 3.236 CP-timed symbol-static Doppler interpolation fixture (G5.10 partial):** Added a four-case native CPU Monte Carlo test for Dopplers 35/350 Hz and DM-RS additional positions 1/2 using exact normal-CP symbol start times at 30-kHz SCS. A two-tap frequency-domain CFR evolves between OFDM symbols; with 1,024 independent draws, the test bounds empirical mean bias, checks empirical/reported variance (linear 0.60–1.05; nearest-neighbor within 12%), and verifies lower noiseless interpolation NMSE for linear in these cases. It does not model intra-symbol ICI, waveform CP/ISI, or establish broad mobility robustness; G5.10 remains partial.

**Edition 3.237 spatial-buffer and time-window leakage grouping (G7.08 partial):** Added a deterministic pre-split utility that links parent subjects when any sampled 3D positions are within a declared Euclidean buffer in the same named meter coordinate frame, or their half-open time windows overlap on the same named clock. Spatial matches use a grid index followed by exact distance checks; temporal overlap chains are grouped transitively. Tests cover cell boundaries, exact buffer inclusion, coordinate-frame and clock separation, touching non-overlap endpoints, registry assignment, and invalid geometry/time inputs. This is a grouping primitive only: no frozen corpus has been processed, spatial coverage/false-merge consequences have not been audited, and the split lock is not externally pinned, so G7.08 remains open.

**Edition 3.238 fixed-delay TDL profile admission (G4.08 partial):** Standardized-record admission now distinguishes the fixed TS 38.101-4 V19.2.2 profiles from scalable TR 38.901 TDLs and checks profile availability against carrier range: A30 in FR1/FR2; B100 and C300 in FR1; C60 in FR2; D30 in FR1/FR2; A10 and D10 in FR2. A10/D10 additionally require an explicit nominal RF channel bandwidth greater than 200 MHz; the adapter stores it separately from sampled subcarrier-grid bandwidth. Native tests accept the supported cases and reject unavailable profile/range and 200-MHz/missing-bandwidth cases. This closes these profile admission combinations, not every standard bandwidth/numerology/test-case restriction; the broader G4.08 scope remains partial.

**Edition 3.239 fail-closed public release packaging (G7.11 partial):** Added a public ZIP exporter that requires an artifact-by-artifact license and redistribution declaration, verified SHA-256 for every file, complete lineage attestation, and—for known OSM-derived artifacts—matching scene IDs and the pinned asset-tree hashes from the ODbL audit. It emits an OSM attribution/license notice and requires a release-specific classification, source-data offer, source software revision, date/ID/conversion provenance or documented gaps, and complete third-party license closure. Tests exercise a complete assessment fixture and reject incomplete license closure, lineage mismatch, restricted redistribution, and tampered files. The actual scene audit remains incomplete, so the exporter correctly cannot authorize a public release of current Etoile/Florence/Munich-derived data; the manifest is an accountable declaration, not an automated legal or provenance oracle.


**Edition 3.240 sample-domain per-path Doppler and ICI fixture (G5.09 partial):** Added integrations.sionna.ofdm_impairments.apply_time_varying_multipath, a causal integer-delay sample-domain channel using per-path complex coefficients and constant Doppler phase over receive samples. A native analytic one-tone OFDM fixture verifies that the static two-tap case is diagonal after CP removal and that a 0.2-SCS echo Doppler produces off-tone ICI within one useful symbol. This closes neither fractional-delay/Doppler-spread modeling nor decoder-level mobility impact; G5.09 remains open.

**Edition 3.241 paired PUSCH receiver impact under full-waveform Doppler (G5.09 partial):** Before the confirmatory run, the controlled-fixture criterion was frozen in `experiments/g5_time_varying_multipath_receiver.py`: on the same 192 payloads, the zero-Doppler DM-RS-estimating CPU receiver must have BLER ≤0.05; a 750-Hz echo-Doppler path must increase BLER by at least 0.20 absolute; and the paired two-sided exact McNemar/binomial test must have p≤0.01. The hash-bound result `docs/research/results/g5-time-varying-multipath-pusch-20261023.json` passes: 0/192 CRC failures at 0 and 350 Hz, 93/192 at 750 Hz (BLER 0.484; Wilson 95% interval [0.415, 0.555]), and 192/192 at 1,500 Hz. The 750-Hz paired BLER increase is 0.484 and p=2.02e-28. Payload seeds are paired across conditions; the channel itself is one fixed synthetic two-path realization (zero independent channel draws), 6-RB/30-kHz PUSCH, 3-sample echo, with no waveform AWGN and decoder-assumed noise variance 1e-5. This demonstrates conditional sensitivity to a continuously time-varying channel combining inter-symbol channel aging and intra-symbol variation; it does not isolate ICI, establish a deployment BLER curve, qualify an RT mobility model, or show a general channel-population effect. G5.09 remains open for broader impairments, channel draws, synchronization, and receiver operating conditions.

**Edition 3.242 CDL NLOS Doppler covariance against a ray-level reference (G4.07 partial):** Added `test_cdl_nlos_temporal_correlation_matches_independent_ray_doppler_reference` for CDL A/B/C on native CPU. For each profile, 4,096 independent channel draws provide paired two-time complex channel samples; the expected normalized temporal covariance is independently evaluated from the 3GPP TR 38.901 Table 7.5-3 ray offsets, profile cluster PDP/angles, random azimuth/zenith ray coupling, and the specified receiver-velocity Doppler phase. The test uses a 30-m/s x-directed UT at 3.5 GHz and a 0.7-ms lag, and checks real/imaginary estimation error against draw-level standard errors. All three profiles pass. This covers selected NLOS CDL temporal covariance, not CDL-D/E LOS-plus-diffuse composition, other speeds/angles, arbitrary arrays, or external channel validity; G4.07 remains bounded to its recorded profile/configuration strata.

**Edition 3.243 G3.03 target-frequency literature disposition:** The follow-up source audit found exact-3.5-GHz building-entry measurements for concrete and coated glass, but they report transmission loss, not bistatic diffuse reflection. García Sánchez et al.'s 2023 facade study uses directional antennas and time-gating to reduce other multipath contributions while measuring building penetration loss; it does not report a bistatic diffuse-scattering calibration. The raw FR3 scattering dataset exposes bistatic VNA Touchstone files for five building materials at 8–12 GHz; this is useful for adjacent-band method validation but does not calibrate the project's 3.5-GHz scenes. The 2026 8/12/28-GHz study also reports frequency-dependent scattering behavior. G3.03 remains open; no cross-frequency coefficient is transferred, no measurement data are downloaded, and no RT qualification run is authorized by this literature update. Details and sources are in `G3_SCENE_FAMILY_LICENSE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md`.

**Edition 3.244 PUSCH symbol-static Doppler control (G5.09 partial):** Extended the fixed-channel 192-payload run with a matched 750-Hz echo whose phase is evaluated at each OFDM-symbol start and held over that symbol. This preserves inter-symbol channel aging while removing within-symbol phase evolution. Continuous-sample Doppler gives 93/192 CRC failures (BLER 0.484); the symbol-static control gives 88/192 (BLER 0.458). The paired continuous-minus-symbol-static increase is 0.026 and the exact two-sided paired p-value is 0.405, so the predeclared incremental intra-symbol criterion (increase ≥0.10, p≤0.01) fails. The earlier 0-vs-750 result therefore supports combined time-variation sensitivity, not an isolated ICI effect; this control finds no qualifying incremental ICI impact in this tested fixture. Both modes use the same fixed synthetic path realization, 6-RB/30-kHz waveform and payloads, no injected waveform AWGN, and decoder-assumed noise variance 1e-5. The experiment calls Sionna OFDM modulation, demodulation, and decoding through the lazy Sionna PHY adapter. The hash-bound v2 report verifies source hashes and recomputes summary outcomes from per-block records. G5.09 remains open; this negative control is bounded to one synthetic channel and is not a deployment mobility result.

**Edition 3.245 corpus-wide provenance completeness scan (G0.07 partial):** Added a read-only JSON/JSON.GZ auditor and temporary-fixture tests. The 182-file results-tree scan found no malformed records, but every file has at least one provenance gap: 182 lack command fields, 180 lack UTC timestamps, 177 lack config hashes, 63 lack recognized source hashes; the auditor now accepts named source-to-SHA256 maps and reports no malformed hashes. Repository-relative declared paths resolve correctly, and no declared local input remains unresolved. Existing files were not modified. The auditor checks required-field syntax and local-reference existence; actual source/config byte-hash verification, missing historical metadata recovery, remote references, and scientific/release qualification remain outside its scope. Legacy-incomplete records are reported without failure by default; strict mode is available but is not enabled against the current corpus. G0.07 remains open. Tests: `tests/test_scripts/test_audit_research_result_provenance.py` (12 fixture tests).

**Edition 3.246 dependency-ready qualification work (2026-10-23):** Release Split Policy v2 now uses stable Euclidean distance comparisons that avoid squared-distance underflow and an exact-ratio fallback when finite coordinate/radius division overflows; footprint validation rejects booleans and non-real coordinate/time inputs. Adversarial split tests cover these numeric boundaries. G3.03 now has a measurement-only evaluator for the frozen 3.5-GHz material-fit margins, including complete held-out incidence-group accounting, separate material/polarization/angle strata, angular-bin consistency, power/angular errors, and prediction-interval coverage/width. Its synthetic contract fixtures do not qualify a scene or provide the missing target-frequency measurements. G4.07 adds an independent 3GPP ray-level CDL-A spatial-covariance comparison for one stationary two-element half-wavelength UT array: four empirical covariance components from 4,096 draws fall within predeclared Bonferroni-adjusted 99% intervals, each with half-width <=0.05 normalized power. This does not extend to other CDL profiles, arrays, polarizations, or measured channels. G5 adds a paired CPU PUSCH Doppler experiment with common payloads and waveform AWGN across 0/350/750/1500-Hz echo conditions and a 750-Hz symbol-static control. At the tested mean waveform SNR of 19.17 dB, 0/192 reference and 6/192 continuous-750-Hz blocks fail CRC; the paired increase is 0.03125 with exact p=0.03125, below the predeclared >=0.20 and p<=0.01 criteria. Continuous Doppler versus symbol-static has -0.0104 BLER difference and p=0.5. Neither effect criterion passes; the fixed synthetic channel remains a conditional diagnostic, and no incremental ICI claim is supported. G0.07's scanner now recognizes the named multi-source hash maps in the two G5 reports; the 183-file results tree has 182 files with gaps in command (182), UTC timestamp (180), config hash (177), and recognized source hash (63), with zero malformed JSON or hash fields. The newly generated AWGN-enabled G5 report passes all recognized field checks. The scanner validates hash syntax, not byte equality. Production D1 RT generation remains gated.

**Edition 3.248 independent-channel G5 receiver sensitivity (2026-10-24):** A separate paired CPU PUSCH probe tests static versus 750-Hz echo Doppler on 64 independent synthetic two-path channels; eight payload blocks are nested within each channel, and inference resamples the channel unit with equal channel weighting. The channel law and two simultaneous 99% family-wise criteria are frozen in code before the run. Static reference BLER is 0/512; all 64 baseline channel clusters have zero CRC failures. The conservative one-sided 99.5% Clopper-Pearson upper bound on channel-level any-failure prevalence is 0.0795, which also bounds mean BLER; this avoids the misleading zero-width percentile bootstrap that would result from all-zero clusters. Mobile BLER is 202/512 (0.395); paired mean BLER increase is 0.395 with a 99.5% cluster-bootstrap interval [0.242, 0.553]. Both frozen sensitivity criteria pass for this explicit synthetic distribution. This reduces the single-fixed-channel limitation of prior G5 diagnostics. It does not isolate intra-symbol ICI, represent a field/deployment channel population, or close G5.09. The result is hash-bound at `docs/research/results/g5-independent-channel-pusch-20261024.json`; RT production generation remains gated.
**Edition 3.469 G5.14 SRS cyclic-shift normalization correction (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the type-1 low-PAPR sequence helper to use an explicit cyclic-shift count and added the TS 38.211 SRS comb mapping `{2: 8, 4: 12, 8: 6}`. Regression definitions now compare the normalized shift phase against an independent scalar expression and cover invalid comb/count inputs. SRS hopping, multiport scheduling, complete configuration derivation, and independent standards validation remain open. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 5.2.2 and 6.4.1.4.2.
**Edition 3.470 G5.14 comb-bound SRS sequence entry point (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `nr_srs_sequence_type1`, which derives the TS cyclic-shift count from transmission comb and prevents inconsistent caller-supplied counts. Regression definitions cover wrapper equivalence and out-of-range cyclic-shift indices. This remains a bounded sequence primitive; hopping and higher-layer configuration derivation remain open.
**Edition 3.471 G5.14 per-symbol SRS group-hopping primitive (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added the TS 38.211 Gold sequence recurrence and an SRS group-hopping index helper keyed by explicit frame-local OFDM-symbol position. Regression definitions compare against a scalar recurrence at first, middle, and late frame positions and reject invalid identities/positions. Resource scheduling, sequence hopping, cyclic-shift hopping, multiport behavior, and independent standards conformance remain open. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_TS/138200_138299/138211/19.01.00_60/ts_138211v190100p.pdf), clauses 5.2.1 and 6.4.1.4.2.
**Edition 3.472 G5.14 SRS sequence boundary and length-30 support (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the type-1 base-sequence availability rule so `v=1` is rejected below length 72, and added the clause 5.2.2.2 length-30 sequence formula with independent scalar regression definitions. The tabulated phase sequences for lengths 6, 12, 18, and 24 remain unimplemented; no full SRS conformance is claimed.
**Edition 3.473 G5.14 SRS group-hopping sequence integration (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a bounded sequence entry point that resolves `neither` and per-symbol `groupHopping` modes into a type-1 SRS sequence. It fails explicitly for `sequenceHopping`, whose eligibility and index derivation remain open. Regression definitions cover both implemented modes and the explicit unsupported mode.
**Edition 3.474 G7.08 RT coordinate identity consistency (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The lineage adapter now requires RT split metadata to carry a finite recorded `rx` and, for complete identities, requires exact agreement with `split_positions_m` before deriving spatial links. Regression definitions cover conflicting physical and split coordinates, and input fixtures now carry both representations. This checks internal metadata consistency only; inventory completeness, external provenance, and signed release qualification remain open.
**Edition 3.475 G4.07 joint delay/four-angle family authored (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a 100-claim, five-profile family comparing normalized-delay/four-angle characteristic moments against the pinned CDL-A–E tables using 8,192 independent draws/profile. The test and seed-collision protocol are authored but unrun; this finite family does not qualify the complete delay/angular law or physical RT.
**Edition 3.476 G3.03 deterministic evaluator-output binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The acquisition-package verifier now recomputes the complete current v2 material-calibration report from the exact bound scorer input and rejects altered metrics or status fields even when inventory hashes and input-hash echoes are updated. The synthetic v0.3 package fixture now uses the real evaluator, with an adversarial metric-tampering regression definition. Legacy v0.2/v1 outputs remain structural-only; evaluator recomputation does not establish measurement provenance or scientific qualification.
**Edition 3.477 G5.14 standardized short SRS sequences (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added TS 38.211 V19.2.0 phase tables for type-1 low-PAPR sequence lengths 6, 12, 18, and 24, completing the short-length table branch alongside the existing length-30 and prime-root formulas. Regression definitions compare representative rows with independent table values; the test file remains unrun in the consolidated-validation workflow. Configuration derivation, sequence hopping, multiport scheduling, and independent conformance remain open.
**Edition 3.478 G5.14 prime-root rounding correction (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Corrected the type-1 prime-root index to apply the TS 38.211 nearest-integer rule for `q = floor(q̄ + 1/2) + v(-1)^floor(2q̄)`. Updated the independent scalar regression expression; the existing v=1 length-72 case exercises a group where rounding changes the root. This is a sequence-level correction and does not establish full SRS conformance.
**Edition 3.479 G5.14 SRS slot/resource-symbol hopping indices (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Replaced the earlier absolute-symbol group-hopping index with the TS 38.211 radio-frame-local slot index and SRS-resource-local symbol ordinal; added the 12-symbol extended-CP slot option. Implemented sequence hopping from the Gold bit at the clause-defined slot/resource-symbol offset, with `v=0` below `M_ZC=72`. Scalar Gold-recurrence regressions cover group and sequence hopping, both slot lengths, and the eligibility boundary. These are authored but unrun; cyclic-shift hopping, complete resource scheduling/configuration derivation, and independent conformance remain open.
**Edition 3.480 G7.08 split-lineage evidence binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Spatial relation keys now hash the derivation version, meter buffer, coordinate frame, exact sorted positions, and component roster; temporal keys hash the derivation version, named clock, half-open semantics, exact windows, and roster. Added `audit_spatiotemporal_leakage_relations` to rederive and compare the registered spatial/time relations against an exact footprint roster and emit input-scoped digests. The ChannelDataset lineage report now includes that audit and uses schema v2. Regression definitions cover buffer sensitivity, changed geometry, exact relation agreement, and tampered footprint rejection. This binds derivation inputs to relation IDs but does not establish inventory authority/completeness or finalize an external signed release lock; checks remain deferred to consolidated validation.
**Edition 3.481 G7.08 footprint inventory in immutable split locks (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added split-registry v3 serialization to embed the recomputed footprint audit, including full parent-roster and exact footprint digests; v2 locks remain readable. New lock freezing now rejects spatial/time relations unless the v3 audit is bound, and a verifier rederives the audit from the supplied inventory. Regression definitions cover isolated-footprint changes that leave relation membership untouched, lock round-trip, altered-inventory rejection, and refusal to freeze an unbound spatial lock. This makes the frozen lock identify the audited footprint inventory but does not establish external inventory authority or signer governance. No validation commands have been run.
**Edition 3.482 G5.14 per-symbol SRS sequence rows (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `nr_srs_resource_sequences_type1` to resolve one type-1 low-PAPR sequence per resource-local SRS symbol under no, group, or sequence hopping, with frame-local slot and normal/extended-CP indexing; its output passes directly to the existing grid mapper. Regression definitions compare each row against the scalar per-symbol generator and verify placement for a two-symbol resource. Frequency-allocation derivation, cyclic-shift hopping, multiport scheduling, and independent conformance remain open; no checks have run.
**Edition 3.483 G5.14 SRS hopping uses absolute slot start symbol (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The TS 38.211 V19.2.0 equations include the resource start symbol `l0` in both Gold-sequence offsets, in addition to the frame-local slot and resource-local symbol `l'`. Updated the group/sequence index APIs and multi-symbol row generator to require `l0`; regression definitions now use nonzero start symbols and reject resource-symbol positions that extend past the slot. This corrects the ordinal-only implementation documented earlier. No checks have run. Reference: [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clauses 6.4.1.4.1–6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.484 G7.08 saved-corpus signed split-lock re-audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added `derive_channel_dataset_split_evidence` and `make_channel_dataset_split_registry` so exact saved ChannelDataset parents and footprints produce a v3 lock. The saved-file lineage auditor can verify a signed v3 lock against an external trust file, compare exact parent coverage, recompute the complete footprint digest, and record lock/signature/keyring hashes. The report explicitly says child data coverage is not audited and remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`. Regression definitions cover signed v3 verification, trust/evidence metadata, and rejection when an isolated parent position changes while its split identity remains constant. No checks have run.
**Edition 3.485 G5.09 retained-result input validation (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Hardened the ordered-impairment report auditor's retained per-channel inputs: seeds must be unique non-boolean integers; each channel record must be an object with exact-length Boolean CRC vectors, finite numeric CFO errors, and integer timing errors. Added adversarial regression definitions for malformed records. This makes malformed evidence fail with a bounded `ValueError` instead of leaking incidental type errors; it does not change the frozen G5 criteria or turn the independent-channel diagnostic into a qualification pass. Checks are deferred to consolidated validation.
**Edition 3.486 G5.14 RRC SRS periodicity adapter (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added decoding for the TS 38.331 SRS `periodicityAndOffset` CHOICE and Release 16 extension alternatives, with CHOICE cardinality, NULL, and offset-range validation. The per-symbol allocation helper can now consume the decoded base/extension pair directly; an extension takes precedence as specified. Regression definitions cover base periods, long periods, extension precedence, malformed choices, and schedule-to-frequency-counter integration. Full RRC message/set triggering and positioning Hyper-SFN gating remain open; validation is deferred. References: [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.214 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138214/19.01.00_60/ts_138214v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.487 G5.14 aperiodic SRS target-slot resolution (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added target-slot arithmetic for the DCI-triggered `slotOffset` and optional `availableSlotOffsetList` selection, enforcing the Release 19 ranges (`slotOffset` 0–32 when absent/defaulted and available offsets 0–7), list size, distinct values, and selected index. Regression definitions cover direct offset, selected additional offset, and malformed schedules. DCI trigger/resource-set decoding and resource selection remain caller responsibilities; validation is deferred. References: [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf), [ETSI TS 38.214 V19.2.0, clause 6.2.1](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138214/19.02.00_60/ts_138214v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.509 G5.09 ordered-impairment DFT-estimator path (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added opt-in finite-delay DFT estimation to the independent ordered-impairment CPU runner, estimator hash binding and auditor replay/source checks, separate v9/v10 schemas, CLI selection, and a fresh-channel protocol. Existing Sionna default-LS reports and frozen thresholds remain unchanged. Seed collision screening, experiment, and validation are pending; no qualification claim is made.
**Edition 3.510 G5.14 SRS cyclic-shift hopping integration (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Integrated TS 38.211 V19.2.0 `f_csh` derivation into single- and multi-port type-1 SRS sequence rows. Each resource-local symbol uses its frame-local radio-frame/slot/start-symbol coordinate and the configured hopping ID, subset, and granularity. Explicit offsets remain supported with range checks. Regression definitions compare against an independent scalar Gold recurrence and cover row/multiport propagation and invalid combinations. Higher-layer RRC decoding, positioning-report integration, independent mapping/standards validation, and consolidated checks remain open. [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.511 G7.08 signed child-binding roster re-audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Extended the saved ChannelDataset lineage auditor to accept one strict, bounded JSON child-binding roster per child kind in a signed split-registry v3. It checks complete child-kind coverage and exact correspondence to the frozen child bindings, records each roster's hash and size, and retains `NOT_QUALIFIED`; report fields explicitly distinguish binding-roster verification from child-artifact auditing/existence. Added the versioned roster schema and CLI parser regression definitions. Child artifact bytes, row coverage, and provenance are still not verified; complete-corpus authority/review and consolidated validation remain open.
**Edition 3.512 G5.14 eight-port SRS TDM mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a resource-local port-activity resolver for TS 38.211's `ports8tdm` pattern, a configured-port sequence entry point that composes the existing standardized cyclic-shift resolver, and multiport ordinary/positioning SRS grid masking that leaves inactive port-symbol REs zero and unoccupied. Both mappers require the complete ordered eight-port roster and retain eight-port power normalization. Regression definitions cover both symbol parities, derived sequences, both grid types, mapped activity masks, and inactive-grid zeros. Complete RRC decoding, scheduling constraints, independent standards validation, and consolidated checks remain open. [ETSI TS 38.211 V19.2.0, clause 6.4.1.4.3](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138200_138299/138211/19.02.00_60/ts_138211v190200p.pdf).
**Edition 3.513 G5.14 decoded RRC SRS choice adapters (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added strict adapters for the TS 38.331 decoded `transmissionComb` CHOICE for ordinary and `-r16` positioning resources, preserving comb offset and cyclic shift as a validated configuration object. Added decoded port-count enum resolution including default `port1`, Release 19 `ports8`/`ports8tdm`, and the prohibition on combining `ports8tdm` with cyclic-shift or comb-offset hopping. Regression definitions cover supported alternatives, malformed choices, precedence of the eight-port enum, and incompatible hopping flags. Full ASN.1 message decode, other RRC alternatives/extensions, end-to-end scheduled reception, and consolidated validation remain open. [ETSI TS 38.331 V19.1.0](https://www.etsi.org/deliver/etsi_ts/138300_138399/138331/19.01.00_60/ts_138331v190100p.pdf).
**Edition 3.522 G7.08 split child artifact byte audit (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a per-kind artifact roster contract and `--split-child-artifact-roster KIND=PATH`. The auditor requires exact coverage of the signed registry's frozen child IDs, resolves canonical paths relative to the manifest directory, and streams SHA-256/size checks through no-follow, single-link file descriptors. Reports distinguish exact artifact-byte verification from semantic child auditing and remain `NOT_QUALIFIED`; artifact roster paths/hashes are self-declared and unsigned unless independently attested. Regression definitions cover the signed-registry round trip and post-roster tampering.
**Edition 3.523 G7.08 signed child artifact roster (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Child artifact rosters now require a detached OpenSSH signature in the dedicated `umcd-channel-child-artifact-roster-v1@github.com` namespace, checked against the configured external trust file and split-registry signer. The report records signer, namespace, keyring hash, signature hash, and the authenticated child-to-artifact byte audit. Regression definitions cover namespace-bound signing and roster-byte tampering. This authenticates roster declarations and verifies listed bytes; it does not parse child semantics, prove per-row coverage, or establish corpus completeness, so G7.08 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.521 G3.03 held-out angular measurement binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Each v0.3 held-out crosswalk row now identifies one acquisition angular-measurement record. The verifier requires that record to belong to the mapped acquisition sample and compares its coordinate frame, zenith/azimuth edges, array order, power semantics, measured integrated-power matrix, solid-angle cells, and missing-bin roster with the exact scorer-input row; angular records cannot be reused across rows. Regression definitions include an acquisition/evaluator power-matrix mismatch. This binds submitted values but does not authenticate acquisition bytes or qualify measured angular distributions.
**Edition 3.520 G3.03 sample-to-file association integrity (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Every file referenced by a sample must also list that sample in its inventory `sample_ids`, closing a one-way reference gap between measurement records and exact-byte file inventory. A regression definition removes the reciprocal sample association. This checks package metadata consistency only; it does not prove that the bytes contain the claimed measurement or establish provenance.
**Edition 3.519 G3.03 sample-to-campaign/instrument binding (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** v0.3 samples now require explicit campaign and instrument IDs. The verifier requires the instrument to belong to the named campaign, the acquisition time to fall within campaign dates, and both instrument configuration and campaign protocol carrier frequencies to match the sample. The v0.3 instrument schema requires a positive configured carrier frequency. Regression definitions cover campaign/instrument mismatch, frequency mismatch, and an out-of-window acquisition. These joins improve internal traceability but do not authenticate equipment configuration or measurement provenance; G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.518 G3.03 calibration evidence attachment (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** A sample must now reference every run-evidence file listed by its selected calibration record, in addition to being acquired inside that calibration's ordered validity interval. A regression definition covers a missing calibration run file. This strengthens package traceability links but does not authenticate the files, calibration result, reference plane, or clock; G3 remains `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.517 G3.03 calibration time validity joins (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The v0.2/v0.3 package verifier now rejects reversed calibration validity intervals and any sample acquired outside the interval of its referenced calibration. Regression definitions cover both cases. This checks timestamp consistency only; calibration truth, reference-plane suitability, clock traceability, and external evidence remain unauthenticated, so G3 stays `NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.516 G3.03 hash-bound specimen material mapping (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a versioned material-mapping JSON contract and exact-byte evaluator binding for v0.3 packages. The verifier checks the mapping ID against scorer input, maps every package specimen exactly once to a controlled evaluator material ID, checks the documented specimen claim and evidence-file references, and requires every held-out acquisition crosswalk material to match the mapped specimen. Adversarial regression definitions cover crosswalk/mapping disagreement. This provides internally consistent, hash-bound package metadata; it does not authenticate the material claim or evidence, establish physical identity, or prove provenance. Packages remain proposal-only/`NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.515 G3.03 acquisition-to-evaluator metadata joins (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The v0.3 package verifier now checks sample polarization, incidence angle, carrier frequency, and calibration against the bound scorer row/input, and requires each sample specimen to agree with its acquisition unit's specimen. Regression definitions cover each mismatch. These joins prevent metadata-inconsistent records from entering the held-out row crosswalk but do not establish that a free-text specimen material claim is correctly mapped to the evaluator's material ID; the package lacks an authenticated canonical material-mapping artifact. Acquisition authenticity, physical independence, representativeness, and external attestations remain unverified; the package stays proposal-only/`NOT_QUALIFIED`.
**Edition 3.514 G3.03 held-out acquisition crosswalk uniqueness (2026-10-25, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The acquisition-package verifier now rejects a held-out `acquisition_sample_id` mapped to multiple evaluator rows, preventing an identical acquisition sample from being counted repeatedly through distinct evaluator IDs. A regression definition covers duplicate crosswalk rows. This establishes an input-scoped uniqueness invariant only; acquisition identity, physical-unit independence, representative sampling, and external attestation remain unverified, and the package remains proposal-only/`NOT_QUALIFIED`.

**Edition 3.524 G5.09 diagnostic SNR-grid implementation (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** The bounded independent-channel ordered-impairment runner now accepts a strictly increasing SNR grid, preserves common channel/payload/oscillator/AWGN random streams across points, records noise variance and measured transmit power, and checks the total transport-block budget before receiver construction. A versioned schema and curve auditor bind the report and recompute retained CRC totals plus channel-cluster pointwise and simultaneous max-deviation intervals across the complete condition/SNR family. This is descriptive output only; single-point thresholds do not become multi-SNR family acceptance. Preregistered criteria, waveform retention, validation, and physical receiver qualification remain open.

**Edition 3.525 G4 independent CDL metadata scalars (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a compact scalar reference derived from hash-pinned ETSI TR 38.901 v19.2.0 CDL-A–E profile tables, with CPU regression definitions for LOS status, component/output-cluster counts, normalized delay summary, angle spreads, XPR, and LOS K factor. No full CDL tables are redistributed. This closes selected metadata comparisons only; per-tap references and statistical/channel qualification remain open. No checks have run.

**Edition 3.526 G4 CDL scalar reference contract binding (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added a dependency-light schema and pinned-source-manifest regression for the compact CDL-A–E reference, including the invariant that a LOS specular component adds one source component beyond the merged zero-delay output cluster count. This protects fixture integrity only; no checks have run and the reference does not establish full CDL conformance.

**Edition 3.527 G5.09 SNR-curve waveform reconstruction evidence (2026-10-26, implementation pending consolidated validation):** Added optional, staged shared TX/oscillator/capture and standardized AWGN draws for the paired diagnostic curve. Every point binds per-channel reconstructed receiver-input digests; the curve auditor recomputes the impairment and AWGN equations from the shared sidecar. Schema and regression definitions cover the artifact contract and refusal to overwrite. Multi-SNR acceptance, broader receiver qualification, and validation remain open.

**Edition 3.528 G6 paired mathematical direct-path ablation (2026-10-26, focused validation):** `datasets.counterfactual.remove_direct_path_component` constructs full and ablated CFR views from one frozen path table, classifying zero-interaction paths as the mathematical direct component. It preserves absolute delays and path coefficients, uses the same ordered frequency-offset grid, records keyed link/frame/port rows, the path-table digest, and the removed-path count per group, and requires both child views to inherit one parent and split from a frozen Split Policy registry. The pair is persisted and revalidated as an immutable content-addressed `EpisodeBundle` layer; bundle admission replays the exact pair from the source path table. The manifest schema now allows the new layer. The focused counterfactual/UMCD/store slice passes 45 tests; Ruff and format checks pass. This implements only `REMOVE_DIRECT_COMPONENT`; physical blocker/occluder interventions still require scene edits and retracing, and no RT generation or training was run.
**Edition 3.691 G7 signed near-duplicate disposition admission (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Report v7 hashes stable candidate IDs and the complete candidate roster. Signed disposition manifests bind the exact inventory and candidate-set digests and provide full one-to-one outcome/rationale/evidence coverage under a dedicated OpenSSH namespace; reviewer principal and trust keyring are separated from verified inventory/split-lock signers. Unresolved, incomplete, or confirmed leakage reports exit nonzero; a complete signed no-leakage review exits zero. The full focused corpus-auditor module passes 37 tests with Ruff, format, compilation, schema, and diff checks passing. This does not verify reviewer evidence truth, trust custody, full lineage, or release qualification.
**Edition 3.711 G4 TDL covariance and Doppler expected values use independent ETSI rows (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Core-1 and configured receive-covariance checks for scalable TDL-A–E, plus scalable/fixed TDL Doppler confidence cases, now derive first-tap diffuse/specular power from pinned ETSI component fixtures through independent reference functions. The CPU-only references pass; native covariance/Doppler qualification remains deferred until pytest and Torch/Sionna are available in one Halti environment. This research-regression improvement does not change dataset qualification, production RT, or training gates.
**Edition 3.712 G1 scene mesh interface is available for independent geometric LOS audits (2026-10-26, focused validation):** Loaded-scene object buffers can now be converted to a hash-bound triangle inventory and queried by the NumPy segment oracle, preserving an explicit unknown result for boundary contact and binding the geometry digest into the result. Fifteen pure geometry regressions pass. The native Sionna screen-scene pytest did not finish initialization within the bounded wait; production RT label integration and material-aware visibility remain open.

**Edition 3.713 G1 generation provenance binds the canonical NumPy geometry oracle (2026-10-27, focused software validation):** Static RT generation uses one loaded-scene triangle inventory as its canonical visibility target and binds its digest/counts in the D1 request, RT run identity, dataset metadata, and UMCD target/run description. Mitsuba and depth-zero PathSolver are retained as independent fail-closed cross-checks. Dynamic NR trajectories re-extract inventory after each configured object translation and preserve per-frame geometry digest, primary state, cross-check states, and agreement. Fifteen dependency-isolated visibility tests and 16 RT mock/cache tests pass; Ruff check and compilation pass. The pinned Sionna 2.2.0/RT 2.2.0 source confirms `SceneObject.position` updates mesh vertex buffers. The native screen-scene test was stopped after more than three minutes in LLVM initialization without reaching the test. This is code integration only; native verification, material/thin-wall semantics, ODbL city-scene review, and G1/G3 qualification remain open. No production RT generation or training was run.
**Edition 3.711 G5/NR receiver reference payload isolated from inference inputs (2026-10-27, focused software validation):** `NRChannelRecord` can preserve exact TX transport-block bits as a bounded, immutable evaluation reference. The standardized PUSCH producer retains Sionna's transmitted bits and binds shape/SHA-256 in provenance; store v4 preserves the exact uint8 array while older v1–v3 records remain readable. The record's receiver observation and `model_inputs()` remain unchanged. A payload-audit decoder reports exact bit errors alongside TB CRC and rejects payload/provenance drift; the CRC-only diagnostic remains compatible with older records. The NR record and streaming-store slice passes 75 tests. The bounded native CPU PUSCH check did not finish within ten minutes and was stopped; runtime integration remains unverified and this does not qualify receiver performance or alter training gates.
**Edition 3.711 G0 local runtime inventory and model-table identity (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added a versioned CLI report for exact lock inputs, installed wheel metadata, Sionna TR 38.901 model-table bytes, source revision, and opt-in CUDA device properties. Six focused tests and both schema-validated report modes pass. The output remains self-reported and `NOT_QUALIFIED`; full compatibility mapping, native-library identity, and trusted attestation remain open.
**Edition 3.712 G0 pinned PHY API preflight and explicit RT dependency report (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Schema-v2 runtime inventory can import and check the Sionna PHY symbols used by the code, and can separately check RT/Mitsuba/Dr.Jit only on explicit request. All six PHY module groups pass; RT is reported missing because `sionna-rt` is absent, with no RT module imports. Eight focused tests and schema/Ruff/format/compile/lock/diff checks pass. This does not attest native binaries or establish backend compatibility/qualification.
**Edition 3.713 G8 estimator-curve fixture and focused metrics suite (2026-10-27):** Repaired the v2 estimator metadata in the unmatched-channel-truth regression; the targeted test and complete 41-case metrics module pass. This restores a meaningful integrity check but does not establish physical independence, complete class-support coverage across model routes, or evaluation qualification.
**Edition 3.714 G8 phase-randomized control data lineage (2026-10-27, focused validation):** Added a seeded phase-randomized observed-CFR model route and contract-v4 schema binding its feature transform and matrix digests. CLI and YAML expose the transform seed; model comparisons use the same split as raw MLP. All 20 runner tests pass with Ruff, formatting, compilation, schema JSON parsing, CLI help, and diff checks clean. This is a dependency-ready research falsification control only, not superdataset qualification. Other G8 controls and storage/release gates remain open.
**Edition 3.715 G4 scalable TDL Rayleigh properness protocol and seed audit (2026-10-27):** Added a pinned-reference native regression definition for scalable TDL-A–E proper-complex moments, with ETSI-labeled Rayleigh tap roster and TDL-D/E LOS-aggregate exclusion. The 95-tap/380-claim acceptance family and 32,768-draw/profile design are frozen. A fresh five-seed roster audit is clear within 2,273 saved JSON artifacts. The source-bound roster test passes; native generation remains unrun pending the consolidated validation/qualification stage. G4 remains open.
